DeepFAN，一种基于 Transformer 的深度学习模型，用于 CT 扫描中偶发肺结节的人人工智能协作评估：一项多读者、多病例试验DeepFAN, a transformer-based deep learning model for human-artificial intelligence collaborative assessment of incidental pulmonary nodules in CT scans: a multi-reader, multi-case trial

朱振辰[1#]、胡戈[2#]Zhenchen Zhu[1#] , Ge Hu[2#] , 谭伟雄[3#]、高凯[3#]、孙超[4#]、周震[3#]、徐克培[1]、韩伟[5]、商美霞[6]、邱晓明[7,8]、谭一清[9]、王金华[1]、周孟英[1,10]、彭丽[1,11]、宋伟[1]、兰歌曲[1Weixiong Tan[3#] , Kai Gao[3#] , Chao Sun[4#] , Zhen Zhou[3#] , Kepei Xu[1] , Wei Han[5] , Meixia Shang[6] , Xiaoming Qiu[7,8] , Yiqing Tan[9] , Jinhua Wang[1] , Zhoumeng Ying[1,10] , Li Peng[1,11] , Wei Song[1] , Lan Song[1]、金正宇[1] , Zhengyu Jin[1]、南红[4] , Nan Hong[4]、于一舟[12] , Yizhou Yu[12]

摘要Abstract

CT 的广泛采用显着增加了检测到的肺结节的数量。然而，目前用于分类良恶性结节的深度学习方法往往无法全面整合全局和局部特征，并且大多数尚未经过临床试验验证。为了解决这个问题，我们开发了 DeepFAN，这是一种基于 Transformer 的模型，对超过 10K 个病理证实的结节进行了训练，并进一步进行了多读者、多病例临床试验，以评估其在协助初级放射科医生方面的功效。 DeepFAN 在内部测试集上实现了 0.939 的诊断曲线下面积 (AUC) (95% CI 0.930-0.948)，在涉及三个独立医疗机构的 400 例临床试验数据集上实现了 0.954 (95% CI 0.934-0.973)。可解释性分析表明，全局特征的贡献高于局部特征。 12 名读者的平均表现显着提高，AUC 提高了 10.9% (95% CI 8.3%-13.5%)，准确度提高了 10.0% (95% CI 8.9%-11.1%)，敏感性提高了 7.6% (95% CI 6.1%-9.2%)，特异性提高了 12.6% (95% CI 10.9%-14.3%)。The widespread adoption of CT has notably increased the number of detected lung nodules. However, current deep learning methods for classifying benign and malignant nodules often fail to comprehensively integrate global and local features, and most of them have not been validated through clinical trials. To address this, we developed DeepFAN, a transformer-based model trained on over 10K pathology-confirmed nodules and further conducted a multi-reader, multi-case clinical trial to evaluate its efficacy in assisting junior radiologists. DeepFAN achieved diagnostic area under the curve (AUC) of 0.939 (95% CI 0.930-0.948) on an internal test set and 0.954 (95% CI 0.934-0.973) on the clinical trial dataset involving 400 cases across three independent medical institutions. Explainability analysis indicated higher contributions from global than local features. Twelve readers’ average performance significantly improved by 10.9% (95% CI 8.3%-13.5%) in AUC, 10.0% (95% CI 8.9%-11.1%) in accuracy, 7.6% (95% CI 6.1%-9.2%) in sensitivity, and 12.6% (95% CI 10.9%-14.3%) in specificity (P全部 <0.001）。结节级读者间诊断一致性从一般提高到中等（总体𝜅：0.313 vs. 0.421；<0.001 for all). Nodule-level inter-reader diagnostic consistency improved from fair to moderate (overall 𝜅: 0.313 vs. 0.421;P=0.019）。总之，DeepFAN 有效地协助了初级放射科医生，并可能有助于使诊断质量均一化并减少不确定肺结节的不必要的随访。中国临床试验注册中心：ChiCTR2400084624。=0.019). In conclusion, DeepFAN effectively assisted junior radiologists and may help homogenize diagnostic quality and reduce unnecessary follow-up of indeterminate pulmonary nodules. Chinese Clinical Trial Registry: ChiCTR2400084624.

该手稿的修订版已被 Nature Cancer 接受（DOI：10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w）。A revised version of this manuscript has been accepted by Nature Cancer (DOI: 10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w).

2

简介Introduction

2022年全球癌症统计数据显示，肺癌仍然是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因，估计每年有180万人死亡[1]。在中国，肺癌不仅是最常见的癌症（每年新增病例超过100万例），而且是最致命的癌症，占所有癌症相关死亡的28.5%，远高于全球平均水平（18.7%）[1,2]。 2017年，中国与肺癌相关的累计经济负担估计为250.69亿美元（占国内生产总值的0.121%），并且预计逐年增加[3]。由于社会经济差异，欠发达地区的患者的这种经济负担进一步加剧，导致诊断延迟和预后较差[4]。因此，及时识别和早期诊断肺癌对于减轻这一负担至关重要。The 2022 global cancer statistics reveal that lung cancer remains the leading cause of cancer-related deaths worldwide, with an estimated 1.8 million deaths annually[1] . In China, lung cancer is not only the most common (over 1.0 million new cases annually) but also the most fatal cancer, accounting for 28.5% of all cancer-related deaths, far above the global average rate (18.7%)[1,2] . The accumulated economic burden associated with lung cancer was estimated to be 25,069 million USD in China in 2017 (0.121% of Gross Domestic Product) and was expected to increase over the years[3] . This economic burden is further exacerbated for patients in less developed regions due to socio-economic disparities, leading to delayed diagnoses and poorer prognoses[4] . Therefore, timely identification and early diagnosis of lung cancer are crucial for mitigating this burden.

计算机断层扫描（CT）是早期识别肺癌的主要成像技术。随着胸部CT变得更加便宜和可用，胸部CT的使用增加导致每年发现数百万个偶发肺结节（IPN），并且随着肺癌筛查的实施，这个数字进一步增加[5-8]。大量的CT图像极大地增加了放射科医生的工作量，常常迫使他们减少处理每个病例的时间，从而导致解释错误的增加[9]。尽管大多数结节最终被归类为肺癌假阳性结果[10]，但在初次检测时准确确定小结节是否为恶性仍然是一个重大挑战。此外，由于CT上的图像特征往往会出现观察者间的不一致，而这在很大程度上受到放射科医生工作经验的影响，现实世界中IPN的评估可能不精确且不一致，导致患者焦虑加剧，使管理IPN的临床过程变得复杂化[11-13]。Computed tomography (CT) is the main imaging technique for identifying lung cancer at an early stage. As chest CT becomes more affordable and available, the increased use of chest CT led to the detection of millions of incidental pulmonary nodules (IPNs) annually, and this number further increases with the implementation of lung cancer screening[5-8] . The vast number of CT images has tremendously increased radiologists’ workload, often forcing them to reduce the time spent on each case, thereby leading to an increase in interpretive errors[9] . Although the majority of nodules are ultimately classified as false positive findings for lung cancer[10] , accurately determining whether a small nodule is malignant at the time of initial detection remains a significant challenge. Additionally, given that image features on CT are often subject to inter-observer inconsistency, which is highly affected by radiologists’ working experience, the assessment of IPNs in the real world might be imprecise and inconsistent, resulting in heightened patient anxiety that complicates the clinical process for managing IPNs[11-13] .

因此，迫切需要一种准确的方法/工具来帮助放射科医生有效、一致地评估肺结节。 Mayo 模型和 Brock 模型是评估孤立性肺结节恶性程度的著名风险模型，但它们是根据西方人群开发的，在 IPN 背景下仅表现出中等性能[14,15]。此外，在现实环境中，IPN 可能无法获得患者人口统计信息和吸烟史。最近，人工智能（AI）辅助诊断工具在将IPN分类为恶性或良性方面表现出优异的性能，达到了与熟练放射科医生相当的水平[16-22]。然而，这些模型主要是针对以低剂量胸部CT扫描为主的肺癌筛查人群开发的，并不能完全代表体检和其他临床目的时获得的非筛查CT扫描[23]，并且大多数没有在临床实践中得到验证。此外，当前用于肺结节评估的人工智能模型主要依赖于卷积神经网络（CNN）及其衍生物，由于卷积运算本质上强调局部特征提取[18,19,22,24]。为了解决上述挑战，我们提出了一种深度特征聚合网络（DeepFAN），并在严格的临床环境中测试该方法。这个新颖的框架建立在视觉变压器（ViT）[25]的基础上，这是一种用于计算机视觉任务的基于自注意力的深度神经架构，通过利用整个输入图像的全局注意力来捕获结节的全局特征。此外，我们的框架中还集成了细粒度 CNN 模型，以提取结节的详细局部特征。最后，采用图卷积网络（GCN）[26]来有效地融合全局和局部特征。这种方法有利于关系学习并超越传统的特征融合方法[26]。Therefore, an accurate method/tool to help radiologists efficiently and consistently assess pulmonary nodules is urgently needed. The Mayo model and Brock model are well-known risk models for evaluating the malignancy of solitary pulmonary nodules, but they were developed using Western populations and have shown only moderate performance in the context of IPNs[14,15] . Additionally, patient demographic information and smoking history might be unavailable for IPNs in real-world settings. Recently, artificial intelligence (AI)-assisted diagnostic tools have shown excellent performance in classifying IPNs as malignant or benign, reaching a level comparable to skilled radiologists[16-22] . However, these models were mainly developed on lung cancer screening populations with predominantly low-dose chest CT scans, which did not fully represent non-screening CT scans obtained during physical examinations and other clinical purposes[23] , and most of them were not verified in the clinical practices. Besides, current AI models for pulmonary nodule assessment predominantly rely on convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and their derivatives, inherently emphasizing local feature extraction owing to the convolution operation[18,19,22,24] . To address the above challenges, we propose a deep feature aggregation network (DeepFAN) and test this method in a rigorous clinical setting. This novel framework is built on Vision Transformers (ViT)[25] —a self-attention based deep neural architecture for computer vision tasks, to capture global features of nodules by leveraging global attention passes across the entire input image. Additionally, a fine-grained CNN model is integrated into our framework to extract detailed local features of nodules. Finally, a graph convolution network (GCN)[26] is adopted to effectively amalgamate global and local features. This approach facilitates relation learning and surpasses traditional feature fusion methods[26] .

虽然人工智能辅助工具作为独立设备的性能指标很重要，但它们的价值还在于它们在日常临床实践中如何提高放射科医生在人机人工智能协作模式下的表现。多读者多案例（MRMC）研究设计是目前在基于人工智能的解决方案辅助下分析人类读者表现的最受认可的方法，但 MRMC 研究具有耗时且昂贵的性质[27-29]。 Kim 和他的同事进行了一项没有洗脱期的简化 MRMC 研究，以证明基于人工智能的计算机辅助诊断工具在提高胸部 CT 扫描中检测到的不确定肺部结节的诊断性能和风险分层的观察者间一致性方面的功效[30]。然而，目前仍缺乏一项严格的 MRMC 研究，该研究基于对发展中国家亚洲人群的临床试验，旨在开发一种人工智能辅助工具来诊断胸部 CT 扫描中检测到的肺结节。同时，人们对一些因素（例如人工智能系统的准确性、对人工智能的敏感性、人的个性）非常感兴趣。While the performance metrics of AI-assisted tools as standalone devices are important, their value also lies in how much they enhance radiologists' performance in a human-AI collaborative mode in daily clinical practice. The multireader multicase (MRMC) study design is presently the most recognized method for analyzing the performance of human readers assisted by AI-based solutions, but MRMC studies have a time-consuming and costly nature[27-29] . Kim and his colleagues conducted a simplified MRMC study without a washout period to demonstrate the efficacy of an AI-based computer-aided diagnosis tool in improving both diagnostic performance of indeterminate pulmonary nodules detected in chest CT scans and interobserver agreement for risk stratification[30] . However, a rigorous MRMC study based clinical trial on Asian populations from developing countries for an AI-assisted tool for the diagnosis of pulmonary nodules detected in chest CT scans is still absent. Meanwhile, there is significant interest in factors (e.g. accuracy of AI system, susceptibility to AI, personality of

放射科医生和工作经验）可以影响放射科医生在人工智能辅助阅片过程中的诊断决策，以促进人工智能辅助工具融入临床实践[31-33]。因此，本研究收集了来自中国九家医疗机构的大量 IPN 患者、超过 10K 例经病理证实的肺结节，训练所提出的 DeepFAN 模型来区分恶性肺结节和良性结节，然后在中国另外三个不同人口规模的独立医疗机构（北京大学人民医院、 2022PHA118-001；武汉市第三医院，武汉第3号QX2023-002；黄石市中心医院，论会申[2023]2号）。此外，DeepFAN的泛化能力在国家肺部筛查试验（NLST）数据集以及临床试验中使用的数据集上进行了测试。此外，为了更好地理解人类与人工智能的协作过程，我们探讨了各种因素对人类与人工智能诊断结果准确性的影响。最后，我们的目标是通过可视化人工智能单独以及人类与人工智能协作的分析过程，来解释人类与人工智能决策中的分歧。radiologists, and working experience) that can influence radiologists’ diagnostic decisions during AI-assisted reading sessions in order to facilitate the incorporation of AI-assisted tools into clinical practice[31-33] . Therefore, this study collected a large cohort of IPN patients, more than 10K pathological confirmed pulmonary nodules from nine medical institutions in China, to train the proposed DeepFAN model to differentiate malignant pulmonary nodules from benign ones, and then conduct the first rigorous clinical MRMC-study based national clinical trial (ChiCTR2400084624) in another three independent medical institutions in China with diverse population sizes (Peking University People's Hospital, 2022PHA118-001; Wuhan Third Hospital, Wuhan No.3 QX2023-002; and Huangshi Central Hospital, Lun Kuai Shen [2023] No. 2). Besides, the generalization ability of DeepFAN was tested on the national lung screening trial (NLST) dataset as well as the dataset used in the clinical trial. Furthermore, to better understand the human-AI collaboration process, we explored the influence of various factors on the accuracy of human-AI diagnostic outcomes. Finally, we aimed to provide explanations to divergence within human-AI decision-making by visualizing the analytic process of AI individually as well as human-AI collaboratively.

结果Results

深度特征聚合网络（DeepFAN）Deep feature aggregation network (DeepFAN)

临床试验中测试的人工智能模型深度特征聚合网络（DeepFAN，1.0版，北京深智博联科技有限公司）已获得中国国家药品监督管理局（NMPA）批准（国药准字20243211932）。其神经架构详述于The Deep Feature Aggregation Network (DeepFAN, version 1.0, owned by Beijing Deepwise & League of PHD Technology Co., Ltd.), the AI model tested in the clinical trial, has been approved by the National Medical Products Administration (NMPA) of China (National Medical Device Approval No. 20243211932). Its neural architecture is detailed in图1Figure 1。 DeepFAN 集成了三个组件神经网络，可增强 CT 图像的混合特征学习和表征。第一个组件是视觉转换器（ViT），它有效地提取肺结节及其周围区域的全局特征，对结节的整体形态和背景进行编码。第二个组件是具有反事实注意力学习和注意力丢失层的三维残差网络（CAL-ADL 3D ResNet）[34,35]，它提取代表结节详细放射学特征（例如密度、毛刺和分叶）的局部特征。与一般的卷积神经网络（CNN）相比，该网络擅长细粒度机器学习，通过捕获小尺度特征，从而增强AI模型感知和区分细微结节特征的能力。在最后一个组件中，采用图卷积网络（GCN）来聚合和融合使用ViT提取的结节的全局特征和使用CAL-ADL 3D ResNet提取的局部特征，旨在全面了解肺结节的特征。. DeepFAN integrates three component neural networks that enhance hybrid feature learning and characterization for CT images. The first component is a vision transformer (ViT) that effectively extracts global features of pulmonary nodules and their surrounding areas, encoding the overall morphology and context of a nodule. The second component is a three-dimensional residual network with counterfactual attention learning and an attention dropout layer (CAL-ADL 3D ResNet)[34,35] , which extracts local features representing detailed radiologic characteristics of a nodule (such as density, spiculation, and lobulation). Compared to general convolutional neural networks (CNN), this network excels in fine-grained machine learning by capturing small-scale characteristics, thereby enhancing the AI model's capability in sensing and differentiating subtle nodule features. In the last component, a graph convolutional network (GCN) is adopted to aggregate and fuse the global features of a nodule extracted using the ViT and the local features extracted using the CAL-ADL 3D ResNet, aiming to comprehensively understand pulmonary nodule characterization.

DeepFAN 是使用来自 8,172 名患者的 11,438 个病理证实的肺结节的数据集进行开发和进一步评估的，这些结节来自中国七个省的 9 家医院，包括北京协和医院等著名医疗机构。数据集按 7:1:2 的比例随机划分为训练集、验证集和内部测试集。训练集包括 5,636 名患者，有 7,873 个结节（1,718 个良性结节，6,155 个恶性结节），验证集包括 831 名患者，有 1,216 个结节（254 个良性结节，962 个恶性结节），内部测试集包括 1,705 名患者，有 2,349 个结节（600 个良性结节，1,749 个恶性结节）。此外，为了进一步测试其泛化能力，作为临床试验的延伸，DeepFAN 还在国家肺部筛查试验 (NLST) 数据集上进行了验证，该数据集包括 7,934 名患者的 17,892 个结节（16,821 个良性结节与 1,071 个恶性结节）。训练集、验证集、内部测试集和NLST数据集中的患者基本特征、结节以及CT扫描和重建的参数如下所示DeepFAN was developed and further evaluated using a dataset of 11,438 pathologically confirmed pulmonary nodules from 8,172 patients, collected from nine hospitals across seven provinces in China, including prominent medical institutions such as Peking Union Medical College Hospital. The dataset was randomly partitioned in a 7:1:2 ratio into training, validation, and internal test sets. The training set consisted of 5,636 patients with 7,873 nodules (1,718 benign and 6,155 malignant), the validation set included 831 patients with 1,216 nodules (254 benign and 962 malignant), and the internal test set comprised 1,705 patients with 2,349 nodules (600 benign and 1,749 malignant). Additionally, to further test its generalization ability, as an extension to the clinical trial, DeepFAN was also validated on the National Lung Screening Trial (NLST) dataset, which includes 7,934 patients with 17,892 nodules (16,821 benign vs. 1,071 malignant). The basic characteristics of patients, nodules and parameters of CT scans and reconstruction in the training, validation, internal test sets and NLST dataset are shown in补充表1-2Supplementary Table 1-2 .

DeepFAN的内部验证和泛化能力Internal validation and generalization ability of DeepFAN

DeepFAN 在区分恶性结节和良性结节方面的诊断性能如图所示The diagnostic performance of DeepFAN in differentiating malignant nodules from benign ones is shown in补充表3Supplementary Table 3。在内部测试集上，DeepFAN 的受试者工作特征曲线下面积 (AUC) 为 0.939（95% 置信区间 [CI]，0.930-0.948），灵敏度为 0.953 (0.943-0.962)，特异性为 0.733 (0.699-0.768)。. On the internal test set, DeepFAN achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.939 (95% confidence interval [CI], 0.930-0.948), along with a sensitivity of 0.953 (0.943-0.962) and a specificity of 0.733 (0.699-0.768).

The procedure involved progressively removing, adapting, or replacing these components of DeepFAN to evaluate their individual impact on the system efficacy. 作为临床试验的补充，DeepFAN在NLST数据集上进行了测试，以进一步评估其泛化能力（As a supplement to the clinical trial, DeepFAN was tested on the NLST dataset to further evaluate its generalization ability (补充表3Supplementary Table 3）。尽管NLST数据集中恶性结节的比例显着下降（良恶性比为15​​.71，而临床试验数据集中为0.92），但AI模型在其他指标下的表现仍然符合预期。具体来说，它的 AUC 为 0.943 (0.933-0.953)，灵敏度为 0.889 (0.869-0.908)，特异性为 0.897 (0.893-0.902)，准确度为 0.897 (0.892-0.901)。我们的 DeepFAN 的阴性预测值 (NPV) 为 0.992 (0.991-0.994)，阳性预测值 (PPV) 为 0.356 (0.338-0.374)。这些结果表明 DeepFAN 可以在不同的临床场景中保持出色的泛化性能。). Despite the significant reduction in the proportion of malignant nodules in the NLST dataset (with a benign-to-malignant ratio of 15.71 compared to 0.92 in the clinical trial dataset), the AI model's performance under other metrics still met expectations. Specifically, it achieved an AUC of 0.943 (0.933-0.953), sensitivity of 0.889 (0.869-0.908), specificity of 0.897 (0.893-0.902), and accuracy of 0.897 (0.892-0.901). Our DeepFAN exhibited a negative predictive value (NPV) of 0.992 (0.991-0.994) and a positive predictive value (PPV) of 0.356 (0.338-0.374). These results indicate that DeepFAN can maintain excellent generalization performance across different clinical scenarios.

我们还通过收集已发表论文中报告的性能指标，将 DeepFAN 的性能与之前的肺结节诊断方法进行了比较。除了最近发表的深度卷积神经网络（DCNN）模型[22]之外，其余研究中提出的方法——即肺癌预测卷积神经网络（LCP-CNN）模型、Mayo模型、Brock模型和深度学习（DL）模型[14,17,18,36,37]——均利用肺结节分析领域的重要存储库NLST数据集进行了严格评估。We have also compared the performance of DeepFAN with that of previous methods for pulmonary nodule diagnosis by gathering the performance measures reported in published papers. Except the recently published deep convolutional neural network (DCNN) model[22] , the methods presented in the remaining studies—namely, the lung cancer prediction convolutional neural network (LCP-CNN) model, the Mayo model, Brock model, and deep learning (DL) model[14,17,18,36,37] —were rigorously assessed utilizing the NLST dataset, a prominent repository within the realm of lung nodule analysis.补充表5Supplementary Table 5对这些方法进行了比较概述。虽然我们努力准确地重现所提出的模型和数据集处理过程，但由于可用细节有限，可能仍然存在一些差异。尽管如此，DeepFAN 在评估 IPN 方面表现出了出色的性能，实现了高 AUC、准确性和灵敏度。这些结果表明，该模型可以作为 IPN 初步评估的有价值的工具，有可能降低误诊恶性结节的风险。presents a comparative overview of these approaches. While we endeavored to accurately reproduce the proposed models and dataset handling processes, some discrepancies may remain due to limited available details. Nevertheless, DeepFAN exhibited outstanding performance in assessing IPNs, achieving high AUC, accuracy, and sensitivity. These results suggest that the model could serve as a valuable tool for the preliminary assessment of IPNs, potentially reducing the risk of misdiagnosing malignant nodules.

The clinical trial dataset contained 463 pathologically confirmed IPNs (222 benign vs. 241 malignant) from 400 consecutive patients (197 benign vs. 203 malignant) enrolled according to predefined inclusion and exclusion criteria (扩展数据图1Extended Data Figure 1）。具体而言，临床试验中心I有166名患者有204个IPN，II中心有46名患者有46个IPN，III中心有188名患者有213个IPN。患者/结节的基本特征和 CT 扫描参数显示在). The readers participating in the clinical trial are junior radiologists with 1-5 years of working experience, including three readers from clinical trial center I, four from center II, and five from center III. Notably, the most pronounced improvement was observed in reader 02 (the maximum area increment), while the least was observed in reader 12 (the minimum area increment).

有或没有人工智能辅助的读者的信心Confidence of readers with and without AI assistance

在临床试验中，每个IPN由读者按照1-10的等级进行评分，其中1-5为良性，6-10为恶性。In the clinical trial, each IPN was graded by the readers on a scale of 1-10, with 1-5 being benign and 6-10 being malignant.扩展数据图3Extended Data Figure 3和and4显示AI辅助前后每个评级级别的良性或恶性结节的数量和百分比的变化。对于12名读者独立阅片时评分为1-5的结节，真正病理良性结节的比例分别为84%、83%、71%、67%和57%。对于6-10级结节，病理恶性结节的比例分别为57%、66%、74%、78%和85%。通过AI辅助判读，1-5级结节中病理良性的比例分别上升至86%、87%、81%、77%和66%，6-10级中病理恶性结节的比例分别上升至66%、75%、86%、94%和97%。这些结果表明，AI模型可以显着提高所有评级级别的肺结节的诊断准确性，对恶性结节的识别具有显着影响。这种增强表明该模型可以提高恶性病例的诊断信心，同时减少误报，从而减轻患者不必要的辐射暴露或侵入性干预的风险。show the changes in the number and percentage of benign or malignant nodules for each rating level before and after AI assistance. For nodules rated 1-5 by the twelve readers during independent reading, the proportions of true pathologically benign nodules were 84%, 83%, 71%, 67%, and 57%, respectively. For nodules rated 6-10, the proportions of pathologically malignant nodules were 57%, 66%, 74%, 78%, and 85%, respectively. With AI-assisted reading, the proportions of pathologically benign nodules among those rated 1-5 increased to 86%, 87%, 81%, 77%, and 66%, while the proportions of pathologically malignant nodules among those rated 6-10 were increased to 66%, 75%, 86%, 94%, and 97%, respectively. These results indicate that the AI model can substantially enhance the diagnostic accuracy of pulmonary nodules across all rating levels, with a pronounced impact on malignant nodule identification. This enhancement suggests that the model can increase diagnostic confidence for malignant cases while reducing false positives, thereby mitigating the risk of unnecessary radiation exposure or invasive interventions of patients.

另外，如图所示Additionally, as shown in图5aFigure 5a，每个读者诊断分数的变化表明，在人工智能辅助后，大多数读者对良性结节的评分较低（平均下降0.65分），对恶性结节的评分较高（平均增加0.25分）。当检查每个级别的独立评分的分数变化时，最初被错误分类为良性的结节（无 AI 时分数≤ 5）在 AI 辅助后显示平均分数增加，而那些被错误分类为恶性（无 AI 时分数 > 5）的结节表现出平均分数增加。, the changes in diagnostic scores for each reader indicate that, after AI assistance, most readers assigned lower scores to benign nodules (mean decrease of 0.65 points) and higher scores to malignant nodules (mean increase of 0.25 points). 分层分析Stratified analysis

DeepFAN、独立阅读器和人工智能辅助阅读器的诊断性能进一步分析和比较，按患者特征（包括年龄和性别）、结节特征（包括直径、密度、位置和诊断难度）以及阅读器特征（包括医院隶属关系、工作经验和教育水平（最高学历））分层。当超过三分之二、三分之一至三分之二和少于三分之一的独立读者正确分类结节时，结节的诊断难度被定义为低、中和高。The diagnostic performance of DeepFAN, unassisted readers and AI-assisted readers was further analyzed and compared over subgroups stratified by patient characteristics including age and gender, nodule characteristics including diameter, density, location, and diagnostic difficulty, and reader characteristics including hospital affiliation, working experience and education level (highest academic degree). The diagnostic difficulty of a nodule was defined as low, intermediate, and high when more than two-thirds, between one-third and two-thirds, and less than one-third, respectively, of the unassisted readers correctly classified it.

分层分析结果（Results from stratified analysis (补充表10和11Supplementary Table 10 and 11）表明，在所有亚组中，与无辅助阅读器和人工智能辅助阅读器相比，DeepFAN 表现出显着更高的 AUC，同时显着提高了阅读器的诊断能力（) show that DeepFAN exhibits significantly higher AUC compared to unassisted readers and AI-assisted readers over all subgroups while substantially improving the diagnostic ability of readers (P所有 AUC 均 < 0.001）。这些结果在整个临床试验数据集中是一致的。特别是，在低、中、高诊断难度的结节亚组上，DeepFAN的AUC分别达到0.994、0.942和0.644，DeepFAN提高了所有难度级别读者的诊断性能（低：0.913至0.956，△=0.043[0.034-0.052]；中：0.447至0.447） 0.642，△=0.195[0.167-0.220]；高：0.113至0.229，△=0.116[0.088-0.144]）。这些研究结果表明，DeepFAN 对结节、患者和读者特征的变化具有鲁棒性，即使对于更具挑战性的结节，也能保持有效的预测和稳定的辅助能力。<0.001 for all AUCs). These results were consistent across the entire clinical trial dataset. In particular, over subgroups of nodules with low, intermediate, and high diagnostic difficulty, the AUC of DeepFAN reaches 0.994, 0.942, and 0.644 respectively, and DeepFAN enhances the diagnostic performance of readers in all difficulty levels (low: 0.913 to 0.956, △=0.043 [0.034-0.052]; intermediate: 0.447 to 0.642, △=0.195 [0.167-0.220]; and high: 0.113 to 0.229, △=0.116 [0.088-0.144]). These findings suggest that DeepFAN is robust to variations in nodule, patient and reader characteristics, maintaining competent predictive and stable assistive capabilities even for more challenging nodules.

来自From扩展数据图7Extended Data Figure 7，我们观察到当读者得到人工智能的帮助时，AUC 稳步提高。同时，结节大小为 20-30 毫米（, we observe a steady enhancement in AUC when readers were assisted with AI. 模型可视化和可解释性Model visualization and interpretability

图6Figure 6说明 DeepFAN 如何利用胸部 CT 做出决策。热图是使用梯度加权类激活映射（Grad-CAM）方法生成的[38]，增强了图像和特征级别的模型可解释性。热图有助于可视化和了解特征图的哪些区域对网络输出贡献最大。观察结果表明，DeepFAN 主要依赖于 ViT 模块的全局特征，同时结合了 CAL-ADL 3D ResNet 的局部特征，代表恶性肿瘤相关的结节特征。方法部分详细描述了用于捕获局部和全局特征的两种方法。简而言之，ViT 分支处理以结节为中心的 128×128×128 mm3 体积，覆盖至少四分之一的肺部，从而捕获隐藏的结节局部特征和上下文关系（例如，结节周围纹理）和血管连接。同时，局部特征分支捕获结节特定的细节，包括密度、分叶和毛刺。例如，DeepFAN成功识别错构瘤的平滑边缘、实性密度和部分脂肪密度（illustrates how DeepFAN utilizes chest CT to make decisions. Heatmaps were generated using the gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM) method[38] , enhancing model interpretability at both the image and feature levels. Heatmaps help visualize and understand which areas of the feature map contribute most to a network output. Observations indicate that DeepFAN primarily relies on global features from the ViT module while incorporating local features from the CAL-ADL 3D ResNet, representing malignancy related nodule characteristics. Detailed descriptions of the two approaches used to capture local and global features are illustrated in the Methods section. Briefly, the ViT branch processes a 128×128×128 mm³ volume centered on the nodule, covering at least one-quarter of the lung and thereby allowing the capture of hidden nodule local features and contextual relationships (e.g., perinodular textures) and vascular connections. Meanwhile, the local feature branch captures nodule-specific details including density, lobulation, and spiculation. For example, DeepFAN successfully recognizes the smooth margin, solid density, and partial fat density of hamartoma (图6aFigure 6a、良性结节），以及与侵袭性腺癌相关的不规则形状、不均匀密度、分叶状和毛刺状（, benign nodule), as well as the irregular shape, heterogeneous density, lobulation, and spiculation associated with invasive adenocarcinoma (图6bFigure 6b，恶性结节）。这些功能为人工智能模型提供了更全面、更清晰的视角来预测恶性与良性。最终，GCN 捕获这些深层特征（即图节点）之间的关系，促进全局和局部信息聚合，从而提高, malignant nodule). These features provide the AI model with a more comprehensive and clearer perspective for predicting malignancy versus benignity. Ultimately, GCN captures the relationships among these deep features (i.e. graph nodes), facilitating global and local information aggregation that improves

诊断性能。然而，一些复杂的结节本质上是令人困惑的，表现出良性和恶性结节共有的放射学特征。例如，上皮增生（diagnostic performance. However, some complex nodules were inherently confusing, exhibiting radiologic characteristics shared by both benign and malignant nodules. 影响人机协作的因素Factors influencing Human-AI collaboration

桑基图（Sankey diagrams (扩展数据图8Extended Data Figure 8）分别展示了 12 名读者以及读者 2 和读者 12 的诊断结果从独立阅读到人工智能辅助阅读的变化流程，因为根据雷达图，他们是从人工智能辅助中受益最多和最少的两个特殊读者。以病理报告为参考标准，DeepFAN 的假阳性率（FPR，定义见方法部分）和假阴性率（FNR，定义见方法部分）分别为 15% 和 5%，独立读数的总体 FPR 和 FNR 分别为 40% 和 31%。在AI模型的帮助下，读者纠正了43%的良性误诊和41%的恶性误诊，将FPR和FNR分别降低至27%和23%。 读者 2 的误诊率高于读者 12（34% vs. 27%）。在 DeepFAN 的帮助下，读者 2 纠正了 68% 的误诊，而读者 12 仅纠正了 21%，导致读者 2 的诊断准确性比读者 12 有了更显着的提高（DeepFAN 帮助后为 88% vs. 73%）。) were used to demonstrate the flow of changes in diagnostic results from independent reading to AI-assisted reading for the twelve readers, and for reader 2 and reader 12 individually, as they were two special readers benefiting the most and least from the AI assistance according to the radar maps. Using the pathology reports as the reference standard, the false positive rate (FPR, defined in Method section) and false negative rate (FNR, defined in Method section) for DeepFAN were 15% and 5%, respectively, and the overall FPR and FNR for independent reading were 40% and 31%, respectively. With the assistance of the AI model, the readers corrected 43% of benign misdiagnoses and 41% of malignant misdiagnoses, reducing the FPR and FNR to 27% and 23%. Reader 2 had a higher misdiagnosis rate than reader 12 (34% vs. 27%). With the assistance of DeepFAN, reader 2 corrected 68% of misdiagnoses, while reader 12 only corrected 21%, leading to a more significant improvement in diagnostic accuracy for reader 2 compared to reader 12 (88% vs. 73% after DeepFAN assistance).

扩展数据图9Extended Data Figure 9展示了使用 Grad-CAM 的人类与人工智能协作结果的示例，展示了三种不同的场景：(a) 人工智能对最初由放射科医生错误分类的结节进行正确分类的情况，从而能够在协作过程中进行正确的修改； (b) 人工智能正确分类了放射科医生错误分类的结节，但由于结节具有很强的误导性特征，放射科医生的预测保持不变； (c) 由于结节具有高度欺骗性的成像特征，无论有或没有人工智能辅助，人工智能和放射科医生都会对结节进行错误分类。这些发现说明了人工智能提高诊断准确性的潜力，同时也凸显了在复杂或模糊的情况下人类与人工智能协作的挑战。presents examples of human-AI collaboration outcomes using Grad-CAM, showcasing three distinct scenarios: (a) cases where AI correctly classifies nodules initially misclassified by radiologists, enabling correct revisions during collaboration; (b) cases where AI correctly classifies nodules misclassified by radiologists, but radiologists' predictions remain unchanged due to the nodules' strong misleading features; and (c) cases where both AI and radiologists, with or without AI assistance, misclassify nodules due to their highly deceptive imaging characteristics. These findings illustrate the AI’s potential to improve diagnostic accuracy while also highlighting the challenges of human-AI collaboration in complex or ambiguous cases.

为进一步探讨影响AI辅助判读准确性的因素，纳入诊断结果、患者特征、结节特征、CT图像特征、阅读器特征等5个方面22个因素进行广义线性混合模型分析（To further explore the factors affecting AI-assisted reading accuracy, 22 factors in five aspects, including diagnostic results, patient characteristics, nodule characteristics, CT image characteristics, and reader characteristics, were included for generalized linear mixed model analyses (补充表13Supplementary Table 13）。在单变量分析中，恶性结节、正确的 AI 建议、正确的独立读数和较大的结节直径与较高的 AI 辅助读数准确性相关（β>0，). 基于网络的人工智能平台Web-based AI platform

为了促进DeepFAN在临床实践中的推广和应用，它已被实施为基于网络的人工智能平台（To facilitate the promotion and application of DeepFAN in clinical practice, it has been implemented as a webbased AI platform (图7Figure 7）。该平台支持用户注册、案例上传和结果生成。当患者在临床医生的安排下完成CT检查时，其信息，包括胸部CT图像，将自动上传至图片存档和通信系统（PACS）。放射科医生可以将他们从 PACS 中选择的 CT 图像上传到这个基于网络的人工智能平台。该平台将为放射科医生提供 DeepFAN 生成的诊断建议，包括将 IPN 分类为良性或恶性，以及结节的特征（如分叶状和毛刺状）。最后，放射科医生参考这些信息做出关于肺结节性质的结论，并向患者和临床医生提供报告。). This platform enables user registration, case upload, and result generation. When patients complete a CT examination under the arrangement of clinicians, their information, including chest CT images, will be automatically uploaded to picture archiving and communication system (PACS). Radiologists can upload CT images they choose from PACS to this web-based AI platform. The platform will provide diagnostic advice generated from DeepFAN to radiologists, including the classification of IPNs as benign or malignant ones as well as the characteristics of nodules such as lobulation and spiculation. Finally, radiologists make the conclusion about the nature of pulmonary nodules refer to this information and provide reports to patients and clinicians.

讨论Discussion

尽管针对肺结节分类的深度学习模型已经出现了大量研究，但经过严格 MRMC 临床试验认证的用于临床决策支持的 AI 诊断工具仍然很少。在这项研究中，我们在中国进行了首个正式注册的多中心 MRMC 临床试验（ChiCTR2400084624），以评估所提出的 DeepFAN 在协助放射科医生在胸部 CT 扫描中区分恶性 IPN 和良性 IPN 的性能。通过集成全局特征和局部结节特征，所提出的基于 Transformer 的 DeepFAN 模型在代表各种临床场景的多中心测试数据集的 IPN 分类中表现出优异的性能，表现出强大的鲁棒性和泛化能力。此外，DeepFAN 显着提高了放射科医生的诊断准确性、置信度和一致性，显示出其使具有不同教育背景和工作经验的医生提供的医疗保健服务同质化的潜力。此外，可解释性研究揭示了人类和人工智能之间评估逻辑的差异，这为临床实施路径和人类与人工智能交互的未来研究提供了宝贵的见解。Although numerous studies have emerged on deep learning models for pulmonary nodule classification, AI diagnostic tools certified with rigorous MRMC clinical trials for clinical decision support remain scarce. In this study, we conducted the first officially registered multicenter, MRMC clinical trial with a paired study design in China (ChiCTR2400084624) to evaluate the performance of the proposed DeepFAN in assisting radiologists to differentiate malignant IPNs from benign ones in chest CT scans. By integrating global features and local nodule features, the proposed transformer-based DeepFAN model demonstrated excellent performance in IPN classification across multicenter test datasets representing various clinical scenarios, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization ability. Additionally, DeepFAN significantly improved the diagnostic accuracy, confidence and consistency of radiologists, showing its potential to homogenize healthcare services provided by medical practitioners with different educational backgrounds and working experience. Furthermore, studies on explainability revealed differences in assessment logics between human and AI, which provides valuable insights on clinical implementation pathways and future investigations in human-AI interaction.

之前的深度学习模型已经证明了肺结节分类的潜力，在诊断准确性方面经常超越人类专家[16-19,39-41]。然而，它们中的大多数都是为了对特定的结节类型[19,40]（例如，实体或部分实体）进行操作而开发的，或者优先考虑局部特征提取而忽略全局上下文[17-19]。其中只有少数经过严格的临床试验验证。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了 DeepFAN，这是一种先进的深度神经架构，包括用于全局上下文建模的视觉转换器、用于局部特征提取的 CAL-ADL 3D ResNet 以及用于混合特征融合的图卷积网络。 DeepFAN 在四种设置下进行了评估：（1）在来自九个中心的内部数据集上，它实现了高 AUC 和灵敏度，并进一步验证了其核心组件； (2) 在来自三个中心的外部数据集（临床试验数据集）上，它通过报告高 AUC、敏感性和特异性表现出令人满意的预测能力。 （3）在代表手术人群的临床试验数据集上，其在各方面的表现优于12名具有1-5年经验的放射科医生； （4）在代表接受肺癌筛查的西方人群的NLST数据集上，它表现出了良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力，实现了高AUC和近乎完美的NPV。值得注意的是，与临床试验数据集相比，DeepFAN 在 NLST 数据集上显示出更高的 NPV，而更低的 PPV。这种差异是合理的，因为 NLST 数据集中较大比例的良性结节可能会导致误报数量增加（将良性结节预测为恶性），并且即使误报数量的小幅增加也可能导致 PPV 显着下降，因为阳性预测总数（分母）增加，而由于恶性结节比例下降，真阳性数量（分子）仍然很小。此外，DeepFAN 还具有可解释性，因为它的内部信息（例如 GCN 节点权重和与结节恶性相关的语义特征）可以可视化以显示决策过程。这种可解释的框架强调了 DeepFAN 卓越的诊断性能，超越了放射科医生和以前最先进的模型[17,18,22]。Previous deep learning models have demonstrated potential in pulmonary nodule classification, frequently surpassing human experts in diagnostic accuracy[16-19,39-41] . However, most of them have been developed to operate on specific nodule types[19,40] (e.g., solid or part-solid) or prioritize local feature extraction while neglecting global contexts[17-19] . Only a few of them have been validated through rigorous clinical trials. To address these limitations, we present DeepFAN, an advanced deep neural architecture including a vision transformer for global context modeling, a CAL-ADL 3D ResNet for local feature extraction, and a graph convolutional network for hybrid feature fusion. DeepFAN was evaluated across four settings: (1) on an internal dataset from nine centers, it achieved high AUC and sensitivity, and further validated its core components; (2) on an external dataset (the clinical trial dataset) from three centers, it demonstrated satisfactory predictive capability by reporting high AUC, sensitivity, and specificity. (3) on the clinical trial dataset representing surgery-intended population, it outperformed 12 radiologists with 1–5 years of experience across various aspects; (4) on the NLST dataset representing western populations undergoing lung cancer screening, it demonstrated favorably robustness and generalization ability, achieving high AUC and near-perfect NPV. Notably, DeepFAN showed a higher NPV while a lower PPV on the NLST dataset compared to those on the clinical trial dataset. The discrepancy is reasonable as a larger proportion of benign nodules in the NLST dataset likely gives rise to an increased number of false positives (predicting a benign nodule as malignant), and even a small increase in the number of false positives can lead to a significant drop in PPV as the total number of positive predictions (denominator) increases while the number of true positives (numerator) remains small due to a decreased proportion of malignant nodules. Furthermore, DeepFAN offers interpretability because its internal information, such as GCN node weights and semantic characteristics linked to nodule malignancy, can be visualized to show the decision-making process. This interpretable framework underscores DeepFAN’s superior diagnostic performance, surpassing both radiologists and previous state-of-theart models[17,18,22] .

准确评估人工智能辅助诊断工具对临床医生评估恶性肿瘤的影响To accurately evaluate the impact of AI-assisted diagnostic tools on clinicians’ assessment of the malignancy

9

该手稿的修订版已被 Nature Cancer 接受（DOI：10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w）。A revised version of this manuscript has been accepted by Nature Cancer (DOI: 10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w).

为了消除 IPN 的风险，我们进行了严格设计的 MRMC 临床试验，以在现实临床实践中验证 DeepFAN。虽然之前的研究强调放射科医生在肺结节评估方面存在相当大的差异[11,12,42-44]，但我们的临床试验表明，读者的诊断表现有了显着改善，各个评级级别的准确性提高，结节评估的信心增强，读者间诊断一致性从一般到中等显着提高。尽管之前的研究[30,45,46]也尝试利用计算机辅助工具来提高肺结节诊断的一致性或准确性，但存在诸如缺乏洗脱期和缺乏亚洲人群等局限性。我们的研究结果提供了强有力且新颖的证据，表明以 DeepFAN 为代表的人工智能辅助工具可以提供一致且准确的 IPN 评估，这有可能减轻患者焦虑、减少不必要的成像并减轻与辐射相关的健康风险。risk of IPNs, we conducted a rigorously designed MRMC clinical trial to validate DeepFAN in real-world clinical practice. While previous studies highlighted considerable variability among radiologists in pulmonary nodule assessment[11,12,42-44] , our clinical trial demonstrated substantial improvements in readers’ diagnostic performance with increased accuracy across rating levels, enhanced confidence in nodule assessment, and a marked rise in inter-reader diagnostic consistency from fair to moderate. Although previous studies[30,45,46] also tried to leverage computer-aided tools to improve consistency or accuracy in pulmonary nodule diagnosis, limitations, such as absence of washout periods and absence of Asian populations, exist. Our findings provide robust and novel evidence that AI-assisted tools, exemplified by DeepFAN, can deliver consistent and accurate IPN assessments, which have the potential to alleviate patient anxiety, reduce unwarranted imaging, and mitigate radiation-related health risks.

分层分析显示 DeepFAN 在各种 CT 参数（包括不同 CT 制造商和扫描/重建协议）、患者队列和结节特征方面的稳健性。虽然独立的 DeepFAN 模型在大多数结节子集中实现了稳健且平衡的诊断​​性能，但其有效性在高难度结节中受到限制（AUC = 0.644），这强调了进一步细化的必要性，例如集成多模态数据或加强高难度数据集的训练。相比之下，该模型在中等难度结节（AUC从0.447提高到0.642；Δ=0.195）中表现出比低难度结节（从0.913提高到0.956；Δ=0.043）更大的改善。然而，它在高难度情况下提供的益处有限（从 0.113 到 0.229；Δ=0.116），这凸显了在具有挑战性的场景中增强辅助策略的必要性，例如提高人工智能的可解释性。尽管存在这些限制，DeepFAN 始终为临床试验数据集中不同患者群体（包括不同年龄和性别）、结节属性（例如大小、密度和位置）以及读者特征（包括教育、经验和临床中心）的读者提供可靠的帮助。The stratified analyses showed DeepFAN’s robustness across various CT parameters (including different CT manufacturers and scanning/reconstruction protocols), patient cohorts and nodule characteristics. While the standalone DeepFAN model achieved robust and balanced diagnostic performance across most nodule subsets, its effectiveness was limited in high-difficulty nodules (AUC = 0.644), underscoring the need for further refinement, such as integrating multi-modal data or reinforcing training with high-difficulty datasets. Comparatively, the model showed greater improvement in intermediate-difficulty nodules (AUC improved from 0.447 to 0.642; ∆=0.195) than in low-difficulty cases (from 0.913 to 0.956; ∆=0.043). However, it provided limited benefit in high-difficulty cases (from 0.113 to 0.229; ∆=0.116), highlighting the need for enhanced assistive strategies in challenging scenarios, such as improving AI interpretability. Despite these limitations, DeepFAN consistently provided reliable assistance to readers across diverse patient cohorts (encompassing different ages and genders), nodule attributes (such as size, density and location), and reader characteristics (including education, experience, and clinical center) within the clinical trial dataset.

经过深入的相关分析，我们发现AI辅助阅读的准确性与DeepFAN预测的正确性、结节的诊断难度以及调整协方差后阅读者的受教育程度密切相关。值得注意的是，拥有医学博士学位的读者表现出卓越的诊断能力（P<0.001）。北京大学人民医院的所有读者均拥有医学博士学位，因此该研究的平均准确率最高（P<0.001）。这一事实可能反映了资源分配和专业发展机会的系统性差异，因为北京等大城市人口较多（根据国家[47]和湖北省统计局[47,48]的数据，2190万对1240万对250万，北京与武汉与黄石）和更好的基础设施，吸引了攻读高级学位的学生，并使初级放射科医生获得更丰富的诊断经验[48,49]。重要的是，虽然 DeepFAN 允许那些患有 B.M.和 M.M.在 AUC、敏感性和特异性方面，在没有 AI 帮助的情况下超越医学博士读者（After thorough correlation analysis, we discovered that AI-assisted reading accuracy was closely associated with the correctness of DeepFAN’s predictions, the diagnostic difficulty of nodules, and the education level of readers after adjustment for the covariance. Notably, readers holding M.D. degrees demonstrated superior diagnostic performance (P<0.001). And all readers at Peking University People’s Hospital held M.D. degrees, resulting in the highest average accuracy in the study (P<0.001). This fact likely reflects systematic disparities in resource allocation and professional development opportunities as larger cities like Beijing, with larger populations (21.9 million vs. 12.4 million vs. 2.5 million, Beijing vs. Wuhan vs. Huangshi according to the national[47] and Hubei province Bureau of Statistics[47,48] ) and better infrastructure, attract students pursuing advanced degrees and expose junior radiologists to richer diagnostic experience[48,49] . Importantly, while DeepFAN allowed those with B.M. and M.M. degrees to outperform M.D. readers without AI assistance in terms of AUC, sensitivity and specificity (补充表10Supplementary Table 10），医学博士和非医学博士之间仍然存在显着的绩效差距。当两组都使用 DeepFAN 时的读者。这表明虽然 DeepFAN 提高了非医学博士的表现。读者们，它并没有消除与专业知识相关的差异。尽管如此，DeepFAN 已显示出通过提高不同培训级别的诊断准确性来帮助广泛的医疗专业人员（包括那些没有医学博士学位的人员）的潜力。), a notable performance gap remained between M.D. and non-M.D. readers when both groups used DeepFAN. This suggests that while DeepFAN improves the performance of non-M.D. readers, it does not eliminate expertise-related disparities. Despite these, DeepFAN has shown potential to assist a broad spectrum of medical professionals—including those without M.D. degrees—by improving diagnostic accuracy across diverse levels of training.

尽管 DeepFAN 在临床试验中证明了读码器诊断准确性的大幅提高，但 AI 辅助读码器的性能并没有超过 AI 模型本身。进一步的分析表明，这种限制与读者的经验、个性或对人工智能的态度无关，而是受到欺骗性结节特征的影响[3​​2]，例如具有恶性放射学特征的良性结节。由于根深蒂固的诊断模式，此类案例常常导致读者拒绝正确的人工智能建议。 DeepFAN 通过整合全球模式超越传统放射学特征的能力强调了其先进的诊断能力，同时强调了人工智能工具重塑传统诊断方法的必要性[50,51]。尽管存在这些挑战，DeepFAN 仍帮助读者纠正了 43% 的良性误诊和 41% 的恶性误诊，将假阳性率和阴性率分别降低至 27%（没有 DeepFAN 时为 40%）和 23%（没有 DeepFAN 时为 31%）。即使是进步最小的阅读器也纠正了 21% 的错误，而进步最大的阅读器也纠正了 21% 的错误。Although DeepFAN demonstrated substantial improvements in readers’ diagnostic accuracy during the clinical trial, AI-assisted reader performance did not exceed that of the AI model itself. Further analysis revealed that this limitation was unrelated to a reader’s experience, personality, or attitude toward AI but was instead influenced by deceptive nodule characteristics[32] , such as benign nodules with malignant-looking radiological features. Such cases often lead readers to reject correct AI suggestions due to entrenched diagnostic patterns. DeepFAN’s ability to transcend traditional radiological features by incorporating global patterns underscores its advanced diagnostic capabilities while highlighting the need for AI tools to reshape conventional diagnostic approaches[50,51] . Despite these challenges, DeepFAN enabled readers to correct 43% of benign and 41% of malignant misdiagnoses, reducing false positive and negative rates to 27% (vs. 40% without DeepFAN) and 23% (vs. 31% without DeepFAN), respectively. Even the least improved reader corrected 21% of errors while the most improved reader

该手稿的修订版已被 Nature Cancer 接受（DOI：10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w）。A revised version of this manuscript has been accepted by Nature Cancer (DOI: 10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w).

10

修正了 68%，反映了 DeepFAN 对不同用户特征的适应性。corrected 68%, reflecting DeepFAN’s adaptability across diverse user characteristics.

我们选择经验不足五年的放射科医生的理由有三个。首先，在小城市的一线医院，由于医疗资源分布不平衡，最初的诊断往往是由经验不足的放射科医生进行的。这为人工智能提供了弥补专业知识差距、推进公平医疗保健目标的机会。其次，中国的两级阅片系统，即由初级放射科医生进行初步评估，然后由高级放射科医生进行审核，可以大大提高初级放射科医生的诊断准确性，减轻高级放射科医生的工作量并提高审核效率。第三，之前的乳腺癌诊断研究已经证明人工智能有潜力在初步阅读中取代初级医生，使这项研究与通过人工智能集成优化临床工作流程的更广泛趋势保持一致[52]。虽然 DeepFAN 在减少不同教育背景（BM 与 MD）的放射科医生之间的诊断差异方面表现出了希望，但目前尚不清楚人工智能辅助的初级放射科医生是否可以匹配或超过经验丰富的高级放射科医生的诊断表现。需要进一步的研究来评估 DeepFAN 在涉及高级放射科医生的更复杂的临床环境中的适用性和有效性。The rationale behind our choice of radiologists with less than five years of experience is threefold. First, in frontline hospitals in smaller cities, initial diagnoses are often made by less experienced radiologists due to an imbalanced distribution of medical resource. This presents an opportunity for AI to bridge expertise gaps, advancing the goal of equitable healthcare. Second, China’s two-tier reading system, where junior radiologists perform initial assessment followed by review performed by senior radiologists, could benefit significantly from improved diagnostic accuracy at the junior level, alleviating senior radiologists’ workload and improving review efficiency. Third, previous breast cancer diagnostic research has demonstrated AI’s potential to replace junior doctors in initial readings, aligning this study with the broader trends of optimizing clinical workflows through AI integration[52] . While DeepFAN has shown promise in reducing diagnostic discrepancies among radiologists with varying educational backgrounds (BM vs. MD), it remains unclear whether AI-assisted junior radiologists can match or exceed the diagnostic performance of experienced senior radiologists. Further research is needed to evaluate DeepFAN’s applicability and effectiveness in more complex clinical settings involving senior radiologists.

我们的 DeepFAN 模型显示出有希望的结果，但也存在局限性。首先，所有测试数据集都是回顾性检索的，前瞻性临床研究需要更强有力的证据。尽管如此，我们的多中心临床试验和 NLST 数据集的结果证明了该模型的泛化能力。其次，我们的临床试验中只纳入了病理证实的肺结节，这一标准可能有利于纳入更多可疑的结节，这可能会影响我们研究结果的普遍性。为了减轻潜在的选择偏差，我们验证了模型在 4-10 mm 肺结节分层亚组和 GGN 亚组中的性能，并在 NLST 肺癌筛查数据集中进行了测试。第三，DeepFAN在人工智能辅助读片方面的功效评估仅限于缺乏经验的放射科医生，他们代表报告潜在恶性肺结节的一线从业人员。需要进一步调查来评估其在经验丰富的放射科医生中的效用，例如高级放射科医生如何与人工智能预测进行交互，人工智能是否可以减少多层诊断系统中观察者间的变异性，以及它在不同临床环境（包括西方医疗保健系统中的临床环境）中的有效性。最后，DeepFAN 仅在我们的临床试验中的人工智能辅助阅读会话中向人类读者提供二元分类结果。提供额外信息（例如三元分类，包括诊断不确定的结节或癌前类别的额外类别）是否会增加放射科医生对人工智能建议的接受度仍有待探讨。Our DeepFAN model has shown promising results, but limitations exist. First, all the test datasets were retrieved retrospectively, and a prospective clinical study warrants more robust evidence. Nonetheless, the results from our multicenter clinical trial and on the NLST dataset have demonstrated the model’s generalization ability. Second, only pathologically confirmed lung nodules were included in our clinical trial, this criterion may favor the inclusion of more suspicious nodules, which could impact the generalizability of our findings. To mitigate potential selection bias, we validated the model's performance in a stratified subgroup of lung nodules measuring 4–10 mm and in the GGN subgroup, and also conducted testing in the NLST lung cancer screening dataset. Third, the evaluation of DeepFAN's efficacy in AI-assisted reading was limited to inexperienced radiologists, who represent frontline practitioners for reporting potentially malignant pulmonary nodules. Further investigation is needed to assess its utility among experienced radiologists, such as how senior radiologists interact with AI predictions and whether AI can reduce inter-observer variability in multi-tiered diagnostic system, and its effectiveness across diverse clinical settings, including those in Western healthcare systems. Last, DeepFAN only provided binary classification results to human readers during the AI-assisted reading sessions in our clinical trial. It remains to be explored whether providing additional information, such as a ternary classification including an extra category for nodules with uncertain diagnoses or the premalignant category, would increase the acceptance of AI suggestions by radiologists.

总之，我们在中国进行了首个 MRMC 临床试验，以评估我们开发的 DeepFAN 模型在协助放射科医生评估胸部 CT 扫描中 IPNs 恶性风险的功效。 DeepFAN 不仅在多个内部和外部数据集以及不同的临床场景中在 IPN 上表现出出色和强大的诊断性能，而且还通过人机协作不断提高初级放射科医生的诊断准确性、置信度和读者间一致性。通过促进跨地区更加统一的医疗服务，DeepFAN 有可能减轻与 IPN 相关的焦虑并减少不必要的影像监测，从而降低与辐射相关的健康风险并避免过多的医疗支出。In conclusion, we have conducted the first MRMC clinical trial in China to evaluate the efficacy of our developed DeepFAN model in assisting radiologists to assess the malignancy risk of IPNs in chest CT scans. DeepFAN has not only exhibited outstanding and robust diagnostic performance on IPNs across multiple internal and external datasets as well as diverse clinical scenarios, but also consistently enhanced the diagnostic accuracy, confidence and inter-reader consistency of junior radiologists via human-AI collaboration. By facilitating more uniform healthcare services across regions, DeepFAN holds the potential to mitigate IPN-related anxiety and reduce unnecessary imaging surveillance, thereby lowering radiation-related health risks and avoiding excessive medical expenditures.

方法Methods

道德批准Ethics approval

本研究包括一项采用配对设计的回顾性、多中心、多读者多病例（MRMC）临床试验（中国临床试验注册中心：ChiCTR2400084624）。它是根据赫尔辛基宣言进行的。主要申办者（北京协和医院，JS-2805）和三个入组和研究地点（北京大学人民医院，2022PHA118-001；武汉市第三医院，武汉第3QX2023-002；黄石市中心医院，论会审[2023]2号）的机构审查委员会批准了本试验，并放弃患者的知情同意书。在研究之前获得了 12 名读者的知情同意书。This study includes a retrospective, multi-center, multi-reader multi-case (MRMC) clinical trial with a paired design (Chinese Clinical Trial Registry: ChiCTR2400084624). It was conducted in accordance with the Declaration of Helsinki. The Institutional Review Boards of the primary sponsor (Peking Union Medical College Hospital, JS-2805) and the three enrollment and research sites (Peking University People's Hospital, 2022PHA118-001; Wuhan Third Hospital, Wuhan No.3 QX2023-002; and Huangshi Central Hospital, Lun Kuai Shen [2023] No. 2) approved the trial and waived informed consent for patients. Informed consents for twelve readers were obtained prior to the study.

该手稿的修订版已被 Nature Cancer 接受（DOI：10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w）。A revised version of this manuscript has been accepted by Nature Cancer (DOI: 10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w).

11

临床试验数据集Clinical trial dataset

此次正式注册的国家临床试验不仅旨在评估DeepFAN在区分恶性肺结节和良性结节方面的性能，而且还检验其在真实临床环境下协助初级放射科医生的功效。The officially registered national clinical trial was designed to not only evaluate the performance of DeepFAN in distinguishing malignant pulmonary nodules from benign ones but also examine its efficacy in assisting junior radiologists under real clinical settings.

该临床试验回顾性收集了三个临床试验中心的 400 名连续患者的人口统计数据和平扫胸部 CT 扫描：北京大学人民医院（中心 I），2022 年 9 月至 2022 年 12 月；北京大学人民医院（中心 I），2022 年 9 月至 2022 年 12 月；武汉市第三医院（中心二），2020年8月至2023年2月； 2020年9月至2022年11月，北京大学人民医院（三中心）的手术量明显高于其他两个中心。这三个中心中，北京大学人民医院的手术量明显高于其他两个中心。为了确保 400 个病例在各中心的代表性和分布相对均衡，我们延长了低流量中心的数据收集周期，同时保持重叠周期（例如，包括所有中心的 2022 年数据）。The clinical trial retrospectively collected demographic data and unenhanced chest CT scans from 400 consecutive patients across the three clinical trial centers: Peking University People's Hospital (center I), from September 2022 to December 2022; Wuhan Third Hospital (center II), from August 2020 to February 2023; and Huangshi Central Hospital (center III), from September 2020 to November 2022. Among the three centers, Peking University People's Hospital had a significantly higher surgical volume compared to the other two centers. To ensure relatively balanced representation and distribution of the 400 cases across centers, we extended the data collection period for the lower-volume centers while maintaining overlapping periods (e.g., including 2022 data from all centers).

纳入标准如下（The inclusion criteria are as follows (扩展数据图1Extended Data Figure 1): (a) 患者年龄 ≥ 18 岁； (b) 术后病理学的可用性； (c) 结节直径≥4mm且≤3cm； (d) 最近一次术前CT扫描与手术间隔≤1个月； (e) CT层厚<2mm且重建层厚≤层厚； (f) 符合 DICOM 标准的 CT 图像。排除标准如下：（a）病理报告的结节在CT图像中无法准确定位； (b) CT扫描范围不完整、CT图像质量差（金属或呼吸伪影）或术后CT； (c) 转移性病变； (d) 排除多余的恶性病例，以确保恶性与良性的比率接近 1。): (a) patient age ≥ 18 years; (b) availability of postoperative pathology; (c) nodule diameter ≥ 4mm and ≤ 3cm; (d) interval between the latest preoperative CT scan and surgery ≤ one month; (e) CT slice thickness < 2mm and reconstruction slice increment ≤ slice thickness; and (f) DICOM-compliant CT images. The exclusion criteria are as follows: (a) nodules reported by pathology could not be accurately located in CT images; (b) incomplete CT scan range, poor CT image quality (metal or breathing artefacts), or postoperative CT; (c) metastatic lesions; and (d) redundant malignant cases were excluded to ensure a malignant-to-benign ratio close to 1.

入组患者的基线特征和 CT 方案如表 1 和补充表 6-7 所示。在模型训练、验证、测试和临床试验之前，所有数据均已去识别化。Baseline characteristics of the enrolled patients and CT protocols are shown in Table 1 and Supplementary Tables 6-7. All data were de-identified prior to model training, validation, testing and clinical trials.

临床试验设计Clinical trial design

该研究采用了完全交叉的MRMC设计（The study adopted a fully crossed MRMC design (图2Figure 2），其中包括来自三个临床试验中心的十二位读者：北京大学人民医院的读者01-03、武汉市第三医院的读者04-07、黄石市中心医院的读者08-12。所有读者均有1~5年CT普通诊断影像工作经验，平均2.83年。为了避免选择偏差，所有读者都是从合格的放射科医生池中随机选择的。有关读者的详细信息可以在), which included twelve readers from three clinical trial centers: readers 01–03 from Peking University People's Hospital, readers 04–07 from Wuhan Third Hospital, and readers 08–12 from Huangshi Central Hospital. All readers had 1–5 years of working experience in general diagnostic imaging of CT scans, with an average of 2.83 years. To avoid selection bias, all readers were randomly selected from the pool of eligible radiologists. Detailed information about the readers can be found in补充表8Supplementary Table 8 .

MRMC 研究包括两轮图像读取，中间间隔四个星期的清除期。第一轮，A组作为对照组，独立评估400例的良恶性，B组作为测试组，使用AI模型进行辅助（全部400例）。第二轮，角色互换，A组为测试组，B组为对照组。该研究平台引导读者用矩形框勾画出审判病例的CT扫描中的结节。如果启用了人工智能辅助，平台会显示一张卡片，上面有人工智能预测的结节分类（良性或恶性）；否则，该卡将保持空白。为了确保准确的结节定位，为每位读者提供了一本手册，其中显示了原始 CT 扫描中地面实况中定义的 463 个结节的位置。然后指示读者用紧密的矩形框注释结节。对于独立阅读，读者为每个定位的结节分配一个二元标签（良性或恶性）和风险评分（良性为 1-5；恶性为 6-10）。对于人工智能辅助阅读，人工智能模型自动为读者注释的结节提供诊断结果，然后读者在提供最终标签和分数之前参考这些人工智能生成的结果。案例阅读没有时间限制。The MRMC study comprised two rounds of image reading, separated by a four-week washout period. In the first round, Group A served as the control group, independently assessing the benignity and malignancy of the 400 cases, while Group B served as the test group, using the AI model for assistance (all 400 cases). In the second round, the roles were reversed, with Group A acting as the test group and Group B as the control group. The study platform guided readers to outline a nodule in the CT scan of a trial case with a rectangle box. If AI assistance was enabled, the platform displayed a card with the AI-predicted classification of the nodule (benign or malignant); otherwise, the card remained blank. To ensure accurate nodule localization, each reader was provided with a handbook showing the locations of the 463 nodules defined in the ground truth in the original CT scans. The readers were then instructed to annotate the nodules with tight rectangular boxes. For independent reading, the readers assigned a binary label (benign or malignant) and a risk score (1-5 for benign; 6-10 for malignant) to every located nodule. For AI-assisted reading, the AI model automatically provided diagnostic results for the nodules annotated by the readers, who then referred to these AI-generated results before providing their final labels and scores. No time constraints were imposed on cases reading.

在整个临床试验过程中，所有读者： (a) 对试验病例的临床和病理信息不知情； (b) 不知道临床试验数据集中肺结节的良恶性比率； (c) 不知道人工智能模型的诊断性能； (d) 在第二轮中没有回忆起第一轮的结果； (e) 没有接受过超出使用人工智能软件所需的培训。Throughout the clinical trial, all readers: (a) were blinded to clinical and pathological information of the trial cases; (b) were unaware of the benign-to-malignant ratio of pulmonary nodules in the clinical trial dataset; (c) were not informed of the AI model’s diagnostic performance; (d) did not recall results from the first round during the second round; and (e) received no training beyond that required for using the AI software.

12

地面真相Ground truth

为确保纳入病例合格，三个中心组成了由经验丰富的胸外科医生（11-30年经验）、病理科医生（15-23年经验）和放射科医生（20-26年经验）组成的专家组。专家组负责最终确定案例选择并为每个案例提供基本事实。具体来说，胸外科医生负责根据纳入和排除标准筛选病例，并将手术报告中的结节与病理结果相关联。病理学家审查病理报告，而放射科医生在 CT 图像中描绘目标结节。肺结节的恶性程度根据ICD-10（2015年版，可通过https://icd.who.int/browse10/2015/en获取）进行判定。To ensure that the included cases were qualified, an expert group comprising experienced thoracic surgeons (11-30 years of experience), pathologists (15-23 years of experience), and radiologists (20-26 years of experience) from the three centers were formed. The expert group was responsible for finalizing case selection and providing ground truths for each case. Specifically, thoracic surgeons were responsible for screening cases according to the inclusion and exclusion criteria, and correlating nodules in surgical reports with pathological findings. Pathologists reviewed the pathology reports while radiologists delineated target nodules in CT images. The malignancy of pulmonary nodules was determined according to the ICD-10 (2015 edition, accessible via https://icd.who.int/browse10/2015/en).

评估指标Evaluation metrics

主要评估指标是结节水平的 ROC-AUC、准确性、敏感性、特异性。测试组和对照组根据结节的具体特征，以及表明良性或恶性性质可能性的置信度得分（范围从 1 到 10，其中 1-5 表示良性，6-10 表示恶性）提供良性或恶性肺结节的潜在确定。根据结节水平的置信度得分，计算测试组和对照组判断肺结节良恶性的ROC-AUC，并进行组间比较。The primary evaluation metrics were ROC-AUC, accuracy, sensitivity, specificity at the nodule level. The test group and the control group provided potential determinations of benign or malignant pulmonary nodules based on the specific characteristics of the nodules, along with a confidence score indicating the likelihood of benign or malignant nature (ranging from 1 to 10, where 1-5 indicates benign and 6-10 indicates malignant). Based on the confidence scores at the nodule level, the ROC-AUC for the determination of benign or malignant pulmonary nodules was calculated for both the test group and the control group, and a comparison was made between the groups.

次要评估指标为结节水平的阳性预测值（PPV）、阴性预测值（NPV）、F1score、假阳性率（FPR）和假阴性率（FNR）。对于敏感性分析，计算了患者水平的 ROCAUC、敏感性、特异性和准确性。在患者层面，置信度评分被定义为单个患者所有结节中的最高评分。患者水平的阳性结果定义为具有至少一个阳性/恶性结节，而患者水平的阴性结果定义为不具有阳性/恶性结节。上述指标的公式定义如下：The secondary evaluation metrics were positive predictive value (PPV), negative predictive value (NPV), F1score, false positive rate (FPR) and false negative rate (FNR) at the nodule level. For sensitivity analysis, ROCAUC, sensitivity, specificity, and accuracy at the patient level were calculated. At the patient level, the confidence score was defined as the highest score among all nodules for a single patient. A positive result at the patient level was defined as having at least one positive/malignant nodule, while a negative result at the patient level was defined as having no positive/malignant nodules. The formulas for the above-mentioned metrics were defined as follows:

灵敏度Sensitivity = TP / (TP + FN) 特异性Specificity = TN / (FP + TN) 准确度Accuracy = (TP + TN) / (TP + FP + TN + FN) PPV = TP / (TP + FP) 净现值NPV = TN / (TN + FN) F1分数F1-score = 2×TP / (2×TP + FP + FN) FPR = FP / (FP + TN) FNR = FN / (TP + FN)

FP、TP、FN 和 TN 分别代表假阳性、真阳性、假阴性和真阴性。FP, TP, FN, and TN stand for false positives, true positives, false negatives, and true negatives, respectively.

模型开发队列和泛化测试队列Model development cohort and generalization test cohort

回顾性收集中国七省九家医院2011年11月至2021年10月期间拍摄的肺结节CT图像，并按7:1:2的比例随机分为训练集、验证集和内部测试集（这些集在患者层面上没有重叠），得到训练集（5,636名患者，7,873个结节），验证集（831名患者，1,216个结节）和内部测试集一组 1,705 名患者，有 2,349 个结节。上述数据集中的良性和恶性结节均经过病理证实。CT images of pulmonary nodules taken between November 2011 and October 2021 were retrospectively collected from nine hospitals across seven provinces in China, and randomly divided into training, validation, and internal test sets in a ratio of 7:1:2 (no overlap among these sets at the patient level)， resulting in a training set of 5,636 patients with 7,873 nodules, a validation set of 831 patients with 1,216 nodules and an internal test set of 1,705 patients with 2,349 nodules. The benign and malignant nodules in the above datasets were all pathologically confirmed.

考虑到仅包含病理证实的结节所导致的良性和恶性结节比例的潜在偏差，我们进一步评估了国家肺部筛查试验（NLST）数据集上的模型性能，其中包括通过患者随访确认的良性结节。Considering the potential bias in the proportion of benign and malignant nodules caused by the inclusion of pathologically confirmed nodules only, we further evaluated model performance on the National Lung Screening Trial (NLST) dataset that includes benign nodules confirmed through patient follow-up.

开发、验证和内部测试集队列的纳入和排除标准如下：The inclusion and exclusion criteria for the development, validation and internal test set cohort were as follows:

(a) 患者年龄≥18岁； (b) 平扫胸部CT检测到的肺结节经过病理学证实，并且可以通过病理报告和/或手术记录在CT图像中准确定位； (c)术前进行CT检查，层厚小于2mm； (d) 符合 DICOM 标准的 CT 图像； (e) 排除转移性肿瘤； (f) 排除影响医生诊断的质量差或结节描绘不完整的CT图像。(a) patient age ≥ 18 years; (b) lung nodules detected in unenhanced chest CT were pathologically confirmed and could be accurately localized in CT images through pathology reports and/or surgical records; (c) CT examination was performed before surgery, and slice thickness was less than 2mm; (d) DICOM-compliant CT images; and (e) exclusion of metastatic tumors ; (f) exclusion of CT images with poor quality affecting doctors' diagnosis or incomplete depiction of nodules.

NLST数据集的纳入和排除标准如下：（a）患者接受过胸部CT检查，并且在任何屏幕上发现非钙化结节/肿块（直径≥4mm且≤30mm）； (b)经病理或随访证实的良性和恶性病例； (c) 符合 DICOM 标准的 CT 图像； (d)排除切片厚度大于2mm的CT图像。The inclusion and exclusion criteria for the NLST dataset were as follows: (a) patients had undergone chest CT examination and non-calcified nodule/mass (diameter, ≥4mm and ≤30mm) was found on any screen; (b) benign and malignant cases confirmed by pathology or follow-up; (c) DICOM-compliant CT images; and (d)exclusion of CT images with slice thickness greater than 2mm.

对于发育数据集，每个肺结节均由三甲医院具有五年以上胸部CT诊断成像经验的放射科医生手动注释。复查医生均为具有十年以上胸部CT诊断影像经验的放射科医师，也曾在三级甲等医院工作过。对于NLST数据集，我们遵循Venkadesh等人的研究[17]的方法来定位良性和恶性结节。简而言之，对于诊断患有肺癌的参与者，委员会认证的放射科医生 (J.W.) 回顾性审查了整个筛查期间的所有可用影像数据，以准确识别受肿瘤影响的肺叶内的恶性结节。 For non-cancer participants, two trained medical students (L.P. & Z.Y.) independently reviewed the CT images using the NLST-provided lobar locations and CT section numbers to locate nodules.一位高级放射科医生 (L.S.) 解决了读者之间的差异。根据既定的尺寸标准，排除平均直径 < 4 mm 的结节。For developmental dataset, each pulmonary nodule was manually annotated by radiologists with more than five years of experience in chest CT diagnostic imaging from a tertiary A-level hospital. The reviewing doctors were radiologists with more than ten years of experience in chest CT diagnostic imaging, who also worked in a tertiary A-level hospital. For the NLST dataset, we followed the methodology from Venkadesh et al.’s study[17] to locate benign and malignant nodules. Briefly, for participants diagnosed with lung cancer, a board-certified radiologist (J.W.) retrospectively reviewed all available imaging data across the screening periods to accurately identify malignant nodules within the tumor-affected lobe. For non-cancer participants, two trained medical students (L.P. & Z.Y.) independently reviewed the CT images using the NLST-provided lobar locations and CT section numbers to locate nodules. Inter-reader discrepancies were resolved by a senior radiologist (L.S.). Nodules with an average diameter < 4 mm were excluded in accordance with established size criteria.

数据预处理Data preprocessing

在模型训练之前进行数据预处理。由于像素间距和CT切片厚度的变化，CT图像被线性插值成三维各向同性图像，体素间距设置为0.6 mm × 0.6 mm × 0.6 mm。 Image patches enclosing nodules were cropped from the original CT images and used as training samples.为了包含结节周围丰富的上下文信息，裁剪后的图像块以结节为中心，但足够大以覆盖其周围区域。在本研究中，使用尺寸为 128 × 128 × 128 像素的图像块。随后，应用了传统的数据增强技术，例如随机裁剪和翻转。然后将增强的图像块用于模型训练。Data preprocessing was conducted before model training. Due to variations in pixel spacing and CT slice thickness, the CT images were linearly interpolated into 3-dimentional isotropic images, with voxel spacing set to 0.6 mm × 0.6 mm × 0.6 mm. Image patches enclosing nodules were cropped from the original CT images and used as training samples. To include rich contextual information around a nodule, the cropped image patch is centered at the nodule, but large enough to cover its surrounding area. In this study, image patches with a size of 128 × 128 × 128 pixels were used. Subsequently, conventional data augmentation techniques, such as random cropping and flipping, were applied. The augmented image patches were then used for model training.

DeepFAN的架构Architecture of DeepFAN

DeepFAN 的神经架构由三个主要模块组成：用于全局结节特征提取的视觉变换器（ViT）模块、用于局部特征和属性特征提取的细粒度模块以及用于特征融合的图卷积网络（GCN）（The neural architecture of DeepFAN consists of three primary modules: a vision transformer (ViT) module for global nodule feature extraction, a fine-grained module for local feature and attribute feature extraction, and a graph convolutional network (GCN) for feature fusion (图1Figure 1）。模型构建的详细信息如下。). Details of the model construction are as follows.

a) 数据预处理。a) Data preprocessing.数据预处理流程包括将 CT 扫描重新采样为均匀各向同性体素 (0.6 mm × 0.6 mm × 0.6 mm)，提取以结节为中心的 128 像素立方块以包含上下文信息，以及在模型训练之前应用数据增强（例如随机裁剪和翻转）。The data preprocessing pipeline involves resampling CT scans into uniform isotropic voxels (0.6 mm × 0.6 mm × 0.6 mm), extracting 128-pixel cubic patches centered at nodules to include contextual information, and applying data augmentation (such as random cropping and flipping) before model training.

b) ViT 模块。b) ViT module.我们进一步将每个 128 × 128 × 128 训练样本细分为八个 64 × 64 × 64 3D patch。每个 3D patch 都经过一个 patch 嵌入块，该嵌入块实现为一个 stride-2 卷积层，后跟一个下采样层。补丁嵌入将 3D 补丁转换为 4096 维补丁标记。 The ViT module also has a class token—a trainable vector prepended to the sequence of patch tokens.类令牌在学习过程中聚合来自所有令牌的全局信息，作为分类任务的模型输出。每个标记中都添加了可学习的一维位置嵌入，以增强位置感知。接下来，将一个类别标记和八个补丁标记输入到十二个转换器块中，生成九个相应的输出特征向量，其中一个用于类别标记，八个用于补丁标记。然后类特征向量通过FC层来预测恶性概率。同时，它也被输入另一个FC层，产生一个64维的特征向量（H”！）。至于其他八个特征向量，它们每个都经过一个单独的FC层，生成一个64维的特征向量（记为We further subdivide every 128 × 128 × 128 training sample into eight 64 × 64 × 64 3D patches. Each 3D patch passes through a patch embedding block implemented as a stride-2 convolutional layer followed by a down-sampling layer. Patch embedding transforms a 3D patch into a 4096-dimensional patch token. The ViT module also has a class token—a trainable vector prepended to the sequence of patch tokens. The class token aggregates global information from all tokens during the learning process, serving as the model’s output for classification tasks. Learnable 1D positional embeddings are added to every token to enhance positional awareness. Next, one class token and eight patch tokens are fed into twelve transformer blocks, which generate nine corresponding output feature vectors, including one for the class token and eight for the patch tokens. Then the class feature vector passes through an FC layer to predict the probability of malignancy. At the same time, it is also fed into another FC layer that produces a 64-dimensional feature vector (H"!). As to the other eight feature vectors, each of them passes through a separate FC layer, generating a 64-dimensional feature vector (denoted

如 𝐻!# 到 𝐻!$)，它捕获输入样本的全局特征。因此，九个特征向量作为输入节点输入到 GCN 模块中。as 𝐻!# to 𝐻!$), which captures global characteristics of the input sample. Thus, nine feature vectors are fed into the GCN module as input nodes.

值得注意的是，本文中的“全球”一词具有双重含义。首先，ViT 模型中根深蒂固的自注意力机制本质上计算输入标记之间的成对注意力，从而从整个输入中捕获综合信息。其次，该术语还表示输入样本足够大，可以包含结节本身及其周围环境。Notably, the term "global" in this context carries dual implications. First, the self-attention mechanism ingrained within the ViT model inherently computes pairwise attention among the input tokens, thereby capturing comprehensive information from the entire input. Second, the term also signifies that the input sample is sufficiently expansive to encompass the nodule itself along with its immediate surroundings.

c) 细粒度模型。c) Fine-grained model.该模块中的输入具有 128 × 128 × 128 像素。经过三个 ResNet[53] 阶段（每个阶段包含 6、9、12 个块）后，得到的特征图表示为𝐹ε𝑅[%#&×#(×#(×#(])，其中 512 表示通道数，特征图的空间维度为 16 × 16 × 16。然后，我们将注意力丢失层（ADL）[54] 与反事实集成因果学习（CAL）[55]进一步细化特征图𝐹，通过ADL进行处理以获得注意力特征图𝐴。同时，采用随机注意力机制生成反事实干预特征图𝐴。接下来，特征图𝐹和𝐴被输入双线性注意力池层（BAP）[56]，然后将输出展平并通过三个并行FC。层，产生三个 64 维特征向量 (𝐻)"、𝐻)# 和 𝐻)&)。特征图 𝐹 和 𝐴̅ 进行类似处理，产生另外三个 64 维特征向量 ( 𝐻[1] )"、𝐻1)# 和 𝐻1)&)。随后，特征 𝐻)" 和 𝐻1)" 通过两个独立的 FC 层，结果之间的元素差异用于预测分叶。特征 𝐻)# 和同样，特征 𝐻)& 和 𝐻1)& 也进行类似处理，将输入样本中的结节分为三个密度类别：毛玻璃结节、实心结节和部分实心结节。The input has 128 × 128 × 128 pixels in this module. After passing through three ResNet[53] stages (each containing 6, 9, 12 blocks), the resulting feature map is denoted as 𝐹∈𝑅[%#&×#(×#(×#(] , where 512 represents the number of channels and the spatial dimensions of the feature map are 16 × 16 × 16. Then, we integrate an Attention Dropout Layer (ADL)[54] with counterfactual causal learning (CAL)[55] to further refine feature map 𝐹. Specifically, 𝐹 is processed with ADL to obtain an attention feature map 𝐴. Meanwhile, randomized attention mechanisms are adopted to generate a counterfactual intervention feature map 𝐴. Next, feature maps 𝐹 and 𝐴 are fed into a bilinear attention pooling layer (BAP)[56] . The output is flattened and then passed through three parallel FC layers, resulting in three 64-dimensional feature vectors (𝐻)", 𝐻)# and 𝐻)&). Feature maps 𝐹 and 𝐴̅ are processed similarly to produce another three 64-dimensional feature vectors ( 𝐻[1] )", 𝐻1)# and 𝐻1)&). Afterwards, features 𝐻)" and 𝐻1)" are passed through two independent FC layers, and the element-wise difference between the results,is used to predict lobulation. Features 𝐻)# and 𝐻1)# are processed similarly to predict spiculation. Likewise, features 𝐻)& and 𝐻1)& are also processed similarly to classify the nodule in the input sample into three density categories: ground-glass nodule, solid nodule and part-solid nodule.

请注意，分叶、毛刺和密度是肺结节的三个重要放射学标志。为了将信息从细粒度模块传递到 GCN，在 𝐻)" 和 𝐻[1] )" 之间执行逐元素减法，产生特征向量 𝐻)"，在 𝐻)# 和 𝐻1)# 之间执行逐元素减法，产生第二个特征向量 𝐻)#，以及在 𝐻)& 和 𝐻[1] )& 之间执行，产生第三个特征向量𝐻)&. To pass information from the fine-grained module to the GCN, an element-wise subtraction is performed between 𝐻)" and 𝐻[1] )" yielding a feature vector 𝐻)", between 𝐻)# and 𝐻1)# yielding a second feature vector 𝐻)#, and between 𝐻)& and 𝐻[1] )& yielding a third feature vector 𝐻)&. 𝐻)", 𝐻)#, and 𝐻)& respectively represent the lobulation, spiculation and density features of the lung nodule, and serve as three additional input nodes of the GCN module.d) GCN model.GCN 模块利用并融合了一套全面的多尺度特征。其核心结构遵循Zhao等人的文章中概述的设计[26] ，它结合了三层残差图卷积[57]并动态更新节点特征之间的边权重。 ViT 模块生成的 9 个特征向量（𝐻!" 到 𝐻!$）和细粒度模块生成的 3 个特征向量（𝐻）"、𝐻)# 和 𝐻)&) 并排放置，形成矩阵 𝐻*++ ε𝑅[#&×(,] 。然后将该矩阵输入到 GCN 的第一层。这里，12 代表数字节点数，64表示每个节点的特征维度。最后，我们使用FC层来获得最终的恶性概率。The GCN module leverages and fuses a comprehensive set of multi-scale features. Its core structure follows the design outlined in the article by Zhao et.al.[26] , which incorporates three layers of residual graph convolution[57] and dynamically updates the edge weights between node features. The nine feature vectors (𝐻!" to 𝐻!$) generated by the ViT module and the three feature vectors (𝐻)", 𝐻)# and 𝐻)&) produced by the fine-grained module are placed side by side to form a matrix 𝐻*++ ∈𝑅[#&×(,] . This matrix is then fed into the first layer of the GCN. Here, 12 represents the number of nodes, and 64 indicates the feature dimension of each node. At the end, we use an FC layer to obtain the final probability of malignancy.

e) 损失函数。e) Loss function.在训练过程中，我们在网络架构的各个模块中进行深度监督。 Thus, the final loss function comprises multiple terms as follows, 𝐿𝑜𝑠𝑠!)- = 𝜔"𝐿"! + 𝜔#(𝐿." + 𝐿.# + 𝐿.&) + 𝜔&𝐿*++ + 𝜔/𝐿-During the training process, we enable deep supervisions within individual modules of our network architecture. f) Model training.所有模型均使用 PyTorch[58] 深度学习框架进行训练。训练期间使用的优化方法是自适应矩估计（Adam）。训练在四个 NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090 GPU 上进行。All models were trained using the PyTorch[58] deep learning framework. The optimization method used during training was Adaptive Moment Estimation (Adam). Training was conducted on four NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090 GPUs.

在训练过程中，我们首先分别独立优化ViT模块和细粒度模块，直到参数稳定。两个模块都训练了 1200 个 epoch，初始学习率为 0.0002，During training, we first respectively optimized the ViT module and the fine-grained module independently until parameters stabilized. Both modules were trained for 1200 epochs with initial learning rates of 0.0002 and

分别为 0.01。在第 400 和 800 epoch 时，学习率降低至之前的 10%。接下来，我们优化了GCN模块，同时冻结了其他两个模块的参数。 GCN 模块训练了 200 个 epoch，初始学习率为 0.01。在第 80 和 160 epoch 时，学习率降低至之前的 10%。最后，我们通过微调 ViT、细粒度和 GCN 模块的所有参数来优化最终的损失函数𝐿𝑜𝑠𝑠！）。该阶段涉及 1400 个 epoch 的训练，初始学习率为 0.00001。在第 300、600 和 900 epoch 时，学习率降低至之前的 10%。每 30 个 epoch 保存一次模型参数，然后在验证集上测试所有保存的检查点。选择验证集上 AUC 最高的模型并在测试集上进行测试。0.01, respectively. The learning rate was reduced to 10% of its previous rate at the 400th and 800th epochs. Next, we optimized the GCN module while freezing the parameters of the other two modules. The GCN module was trained for 200 epochs with an initial learning rate of 0.01. and the learning rate was reduced to 10% of its previous rate at the 80th and 160th epochs. Finally, we optimized the final loss function 𝐿𝑜𝑠𝑠!)- by fine-tuning all the parameters of the ViT, fine-grained, and GCN modules. This phase involved training for 1400 epochs with an initial learning rate of 0.00001. The learning rate was reduced to 10% of its previous rate at the 300th, 600th, and 900th epochs. Model parameters were saved every 30 epochs, and then all saved checkpoints were tested on the validation set. The model with the highest AUC on the validation set was selected and tested on the testing set.

统计和再现性Statistics and reproducibility

样本量是通过 Kevin M. Schartz 和 Stephen L. Hillis[59] 开发的多读者诊断测试研究软件，通过 AUROC 的优越性测试确定的，详细信息参见样本量估计The sample size was determined with the superiority test of AUROC via the multi-reader diagnostic test research software developed by Kevin M. Schartz and Stephen L. Hillis[59] as detailed in sample size estimation in the补充信息Supplementary Information。这是一项单盲、多读者诊断研究。读者对所有临床、病理和结果信息以及人工智能模型的诊断性能都一无所知。数据分析由不参与图像解释的研究人员独立进行。使用 AUC、敏感性、特异性、准确性、PPV、NPV 和 F1 分数评估 AI 模型或读取器的诊断性能，CI 为 95%。 95% CI 是通过非参数引导法通过 1,000 个重采样事件计算得出的。 DeepFAN 确定恶性肿瘤的阈值被设置为在验证集上优化模型性能（F1 分数）的值。该阈值一致应用于所有测试集（内部测试集和 NLST 测试集）和临床试验数据集。读者的 ROC 曲线（有或没有 DeepFAN 辅助）是根据他们分配给结节的恶性评分生成的，而敏感性和特异性是使用读者提供的二进制标签计算的。观察者间的诊断一致性是用 Cohen 的 kappa 来衡量的。使用逻辑回归模型分析结节特征与 AI 恶性肿瘤预测之间的关系。采用广义线性混合模型探讨影响人机协作准确性的因素，以读者和案例作为随机效应，其他因素作为固定效应。. This was a single-blind, multi-reader diagnostic study. Readers were blinded to all clinical, pathological, and outcome information as well as to the AI model’s diagnostic performance. Data analysis was performed independently by investigators who were not involved in image interpretation. The diagnostic performance of the AI model or readers was assessed using AUC, sensitivity, specificity, accuracy, PPV, NPV, and F1-score, with 95% CI. The 95% CI was calculated through the nonparametric bootstrap method with 1,000 resampling events. The threshold for DeepFAN to determine malignancy was set to the value that optimizes the model’s performance (F1-score) over the validation set. This threshold was consistently applied across all test sets (internal test set and NLST test set) and clinical trial dataset. The ROC curves for readers (with and without DeepFAN assistance) were generated according to the malignancy scores they assigned to the nodules, while sensitivity and specificity were calculated using the binary labels provided by the readers. Interobserver diagnostic agreement was measured with Cohen's kappa. A logistic regression model was used to analyze the relationship between nodule characteristics and AI malignancy predictions. The generalized linear mixed model was used to explore factors influencing the accuracy of human-AI collaboration, with readers and cases as random effects and other factors as fixed effects.

使用适当的统计测试来分析数据，如每个图和表图例中所述。通过将统计检验与图形方法相结合来评估正态性，并酌情评估方差同质性。除非另有说明，所有测试都是双面的，并且Appropriate statistical tests were used to analyze data, as described in each figure and table legend. Normality was evaluated by combining statistical tests with graphical methods, accompanied by an assessment of homogeneity of variances as appropriate. Unless otherwise specified, all tests were two-sided, andP小于 0.05 的值被认为具有统计显着性。应用 Bonferroni 方法来调整多重比较。使用 R（版本 4.4.1）和 Python（版本 3.6.13）进行统计分析。使用PyTorch（版本1.10.0）深度学习框架进行数据预处理和模型开发。任何分析均未排除读者评价数据。在 MRMC 临床试验期间，所有读者都是从合格的放射科医生池中随机选择的，随后以均匀的方式随机分配到对照组或测试组。请参阅“values less than 0.05 were considered statistically significant. The Bonferroni method was applied to adjust for multiple comparisons. Statistical analyses were performed using R (version 4.4.1) and Python (version 3.6.13). Data preprocessing and model development were conducted using the PyTorch (version 1.10.0) deep learning framework. No reader-evaluation data were excluded from any analysis. During the MRMC clinical trial, all readers were randomly selected from the pool of eligible radiologists and were subsequently randomized in an even manner to either the control or test group. Please refer to the “临床试验设计”Clinical trial design”部分了解更多详细信息。section for more details.

数据可用性Data availability

由于道德和研究限制，无法获得专有的培训和临床试验数据集。 NLST 数据集（放射学 CT 图像和临床数据，包括数据字典）可从 https://www.cancerimagingarchive.net/collection/nlst/ 获取。此外，本研究中分析的 NLST 子集的患者 ID 可在 GitHub 上获取：https://github.com/zhjtwx/DeepFAN/blob/main/sample_data/Filtered_NLST_Subset.csv。其他支持数据可根据要求从通讯作者（Lan Song，songl@pumch.cn；Yizhou Yu，yizhouy@acm.org）处获得，但须经机构批准和 6 周的审查。本文提供了源数据。Proprietary training and clinical trial datasets are unavailable due to ethical and research restrictions. The NLST dataset (Radiology CT Images and Clinical data including data dictionaries) is available at https://www.cancerimagingarchive.net/collection/nlst/. Additionally, the Patient IDs of the NLST subset analyzed in this study is available on GitHub at https://github.com/zhjtwx/DeepFAN/blob/main/sample_data/Filtered_NLST_Subset.csv. Other supporting data are available from the corresponding authors (Lan Song, songl@pumch.cn; Yizhou Yu, yizhouy@acm.org) upon request, subject to institutional approval and a 6-week review. Source data are provided with this paper.

图 1. DeepFAN 的神经架构。Figure 1. Neural architecture of DeepFAN.

DeepFAN集成了三个模块。第一个模块利用ViT有效捕获肺结节及其周围环境的全局特征，代表结节的整体形态和分布特征。第二个模块采用细粒度 CAL-ADL 3D ResNet，提取代表结节详细放射学特征的特征。最后一步，引入GCN来学习ViT提取的全局特征和CAL-ADL 3D ResNet提取的局部特征之间的关系，旨在全面了解病理分类和肺结节特征之间的相关性。 DeepFAN 是使用 5,636 名患者的训练集开发的，并且 DeepFAN 架构的超参数在 831 名患者的验证集上进行了调整。 DeepFAN 的性能在包含 1,705 名患者的独立内部测试集、包含 400 名患者的多中心临床试验测试集以及包含 7,934 名患者的 NLST 数据集上进行评估。粗体数字代表结节的数量。DeepFAN integrates three modules. The first module utilizes a ViT to effectively capture global features of pulmonary nodules and their surroundings, representing the overall morphology and distribution characteristics of the nodules. The second module employs a fine-grained CAL-ADL 3D ResNet, which extracts features representing detailed radiologic characteristics of the nodules. In the last step, a GCN is introduced to learn the relationships between the global features extracted using ViT and the local features extracted using CAL-ADL 3D ResNet, aiming to comprehensively understand the correlation between pathological classification and pulmonary nodule characterization. DeepFAN was developed using a training set of 5,636 patients and the hyperparameters of the DeepFAN architecture were tuned on a validation set of 831 patients. The performance of DeepFAN was evaluated on an independent internal test set of 1,705 patients, a multicenter clinical trial test set of 400 patients and the NLST dataset of 7,934 patients. Boldface numbers represent numbers of nodules.

缩写：Abbreviations:ViT = 视觉变换器，CAL = 反事实注意力学习，ADL = 注意力丢失层，3D = 三维，ResNet = 残差网络，GCN = 图卷积网络，NLST = 国家肺部筛查试验，BAP = 双线性注意力池，FC = 全连接，Dim = 维度，C = 通道，：减法。ViT = vision transformer, CAL = counterfactual attention learning, ADL = attention dropout layer, 3D = three-dimensional, ResNet = residual network, GCN = graph convolution network, NLST = national lung screening trial, BAP = bilinear attention pooling, FC = fully connected, Dim = dimensions, C = channel, : subtract.

图 2. 临床试验工作流程。Figure 2. Clinical trial workflow.

该研究包括来自具有国家临床试验资格的医院的三个回顾性数据集（400名患者，463个IPN）。从同一三个机构中选出的 12 名读者被随机分配到 A 组和 B 组，进行配对 CT 图像评估。 MRMC 程序包括两轮阅读，中间有 4 周的冲洗期。第一阶段，A组作为对照组，在没有AI的情况下评估病例的良恶性，B组作为测试组，使用AI（DeepFAN）辅助评估。第二阶段，角色互换，A组为试验组，B组为对照组。基于胸外科医生、病理学家和放射科医生合作开发的金标准参考库，对人工智能模型、个体读者和人工智能辅助读者的诊断性能进行了分析和比较。The study included three retrospective datasets (400 patients with 463 IPNs) from hospitals with national clinical trial qualifications. Twelve readers selected from the same three institutions were randomly assigned to group A and B for paired CT image assessment. The MRMC procedure consisted of two reading rounds, separated by a 4-week washout period. In the first phase, group A served as the control group, assessing the benignity and malignancy of the cases without AI, while Group B served as the test group, using AI (DeepFAN) to assist assessment. In the second phase, the roles were reversed, with group A acting as the test group and group B as the control group. Based on a gold standard reference library developed collaboratively by thoracic surgeons, pathologists, and radiologists, the diagnostic performance of the AI model, individual readers, and AI-assisted readers were analyzed and compared.

缩写：Abbreviations:AI = 人工智能，IPN = 偶发肺结节，CT = 计算机断层扫描，MRMC = 多读器多病例。AI = artificial intelligence, IPN = incidental pulmonary nodule, CT = computed tomography, MRMC = multireader multicase.
