ROC curves of DeepFAN and performance of readers with and without DeepFAN assistance.四个子图分别代表 DeepFAN 和 12 位读者对三个临床试验中心的整体组合数据集和每个中心的单独数据集的诊断性能。 ROC曲线代表DeepFAN的性能，曲线上的红点表示与二进制截止值相对应的操作点。每个箭头底部的点代表在没有 DeepFAN 辅助的情况下每个读取器的性能，而箭头则表示在 DeepFAN 辅助下读取器性能的变化。图例中的值是 AUC，括号内为 95% CI。The four subplots represent the diagnostic performance of DeepFAN and the 12 readers on both the overall combined dataset from the three clinical trial centers and each center’s individual dataset, respectively. 图 5.人工智能辅助阅读的诊断变化以及 12 名读者的诊断一致性。Figure 5. Diagnostic change with AI-assisted reading and diagnostic agreement for the twelve readers.(a) 横轴表示不同阅读器（左图）和无辅助阅读的诊断分数（右图），而纵轴表示诊断分数的平均差异，计算方法是首先从有 DeepFAN 辅助的诊断分数中减去没有 DeepFAN 辅助的诊断分数，然后对分数差进行平均。实际数据点的数量如下表所示。 (b,c)图中所示的数字代表kappa值。这些 kappa 值解释如下：<0.2，一致性差； 0.21-0.4，一致性一般； 0.41-0.6，稠度中等； 0.61-0.8，基本一致； 0.811.0，几乎完美的一致性。(a) The horizontal axis indicates different readers (left panel) and diagnostic score of unassisted reading (right panel), while the vertical axis indicates the mean differences of diagnostic scores calculated by first subtracting diagnostic scores assigned without DeepFAN assistance from those assigned with DeepFAN assistance and then averaging the score differences. The number of actual data points was illustrated in the tables below. (b, c) The numbers shown in the figure represent kappa values. These kappa values are interpreted as follows: <0.2, poor consistency; 0.21-0.4, fair consistency; 0.41-0.6, moderate consistency; 0.61-0.8, substantial consistency; 0.811.0, almost perfect consistency.

缩写：Abbreviations:AI=人工智能（DeepFAN）。AI = artificial intelligence (DeepFAN).

图 6. 临床试验中 DeepFAN 区分恶性肺结节和良性结节的机制可视化。Figure 6. Visualization of the mechanism of DeepFAN in differentiating malignant pulmonary nodules from benign ones in clinical trials.

(a) DeepFAN 正确分类的良性结节（错构瘤），边缘光滑且脂肪密度高。 （二）A(a) A benign nodule (hamartoma) correctly classified by DeepFAN, with a smooth margin and fat density. (b) A

该手稿的修订版已被 Nature Cancer 接受（DOI：10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w）。A revised version of this manuscript has been accepted by Nature Cancer (DOI: 10.1038/s43018-026-01147-w).

24

DeepFAN 正确分类的恶性结节（侵袭性腺癌），其特征为形状不规则、密度不均匀、分叶状和毛刺状。 (c) DeepFAN 错误分类的良性结节（上皮增生），显示分散的形态、不均匀的毛玻璃密度和邻近的血管分支。 (d) DeepFAN 误分类的恶性结节（浸润性腺癌），形状不规则、分叶状、毛刺状、实性密度，难以与肉芽肿性炎症鉴别。malignant nodule (invasive adenocarcinoma) correctly classified by DeepFAN, characterized by irregular shape, heterogeneous density, lobulation, and spiculation. (c) A benign nodule (epithelial hyperplasia) misclassified by DeepFAN, showing dispersed morphology, heterogeneous ground-glass density, and adjacent vascular branches. (d) A malignant nodule (invasive adenocarcinoma) misclassified by DeepFAN, with irregular shape, lobulation, spiculation, and solid density, making it difficult to differentiate from granulomatous inflammation.

在每个子图中，顶行显示原始胸部 CT、放大的结节图像以及通过在原始图像上叠加彩色注意力图生成的相应类激活图。深红色区域表示 DeepFAN 的注意力增强，而深蓝色区域表示注意力减少。下面的虚线框显示了 DeepFAN 中每个模块的热图。 ViT 捕获结节及其周围环境的全局特征，CAL-ADL 3D ResNet 提取代表性局部特征（分叶、毛刺和密度），GCN 用于特征融合。不同的特征（节点）对最终的预测结果有不同的贡献（权重），最终的预测结果是使用 GCN 模型第一输入层的梯度、特征值和边权重计算得出的。随后，执行平均和归一化，为 12 个节点生成 12 个值。值得注意的是，全局权重是通过将与 ViT 相关的 9 个节点的特征权重相加来确定的，与其他节点相比，该权重是最大的。In each subplot, the top row shows the original chest CT, magnified nodule images, and corresponding class activation maps generated by overlaying colored attention maps on the original image. Darker red regions signify heightened attention from DeepFAN, while darker blue regions denote reduced attention. The dashed box below shows heatmaps of each module in DeepFAN. ViT captures global features of the nodules and their surroundings, CAL-ADL 3D ResNet extracts representative local features (lobulation, spiculation, and density), and GCN is used for feature fusion. Different features (nodes) have different contributions (weights) to the final prediction result, which is calculated using the gradient, the feature value and edge weights from the first input layer of the GCN model. Subsequently, averaging and normalization are performed, yielding 12 values for 12 nodes. Notably, the global weight is determined by summing the feature weights of the 9 nodes associated with the ViT, which is the largest compared to the other nodes.

缩写：Abbreviations:ViT = 视觉变换器，CAL = 反事实注意力学习，ADL = 注意力丢失层，3D = 三维，ResNet = 残差网络，GCN = 图卷积网络。ViT = vision transformer, CAL = counterfactual attention learning, ADL = attention dropout layer, 3D = three-dimensional, ResNet = residual network, GCN = graph convolution network.

恶性评分Malignant Score
良性Benign
忠告Advice
放射科医生基于网络的诊断平台Radiologists Web-based Diagnostic Platform
----- 图片文字结束---------- End of picture text -----

图7.基于网络的AI平台辅助肺结节的诊断。Figure 7. Web-based AI platform for assisting the diagnosis of pulmonary nodules.

当患者在临床医生的安排下完成CT检查时，包括胸部CT图像在内的患者信息将自动上传至PACS。放射科医生可以将相应的 CT 图像从 PACS 上传到基于网络的 AI 平台。基于DeepFAN模型，该平台将为放射科医生提供诊断建议，包括肺结节的良恶性分类以及分叶状和毛刺状等结节的特征。最后，放射科医生参考这些信息后得出有关肺结节性质的结论，并将报告提供给患者和临床医生。When patients complete a CT examination under the arrangement of clinicians, their information, including chest CT images will be automatically uploaded to the PACS. Radiologists can upload corresponding CT images from PACS to the web-based AI platform. Based on the DeepFAN model, the platform will provide diagnostic advice to radiologists, including the benign and malignant classification of pulmonary nodules and the characteristics of nodules such as lobulation and spiculation. Finally, radiologists make the conclusion about the nature of pulmonary nodules after referring to this information and provide the reports to patients and clinicians.

缩写：Abbreviations:AI = 人工智能，CT = 计算机断层扫描，PACS = 图片存档和通信系统。AI = artificial intelligence, CT = computed tomography, PACS = picture archiving and communication system.

Unless otherwise, pairwise comparisons among AI predictions, 每个子组的独立预测和人工智能辅助预测显示，所有绩效指标的 a_P_ 值均小于 0.01。 DeLong 测试用于unassisted predictions, and AI-assisted predictions over each subgroup showed a_P_-value of less than 0.01 for all performance measures. The DeLong test was used to 比较配对组之间的 AUC，同时采用 McNemar 检验来评估敏感性和特异性的差异。对于涉及两个以上的比较compare AUCs between paired groups, while McNemar's test was employed to assess differences in sensitivity and specificity. For comparisons involving more than two 组中，应用 Bonferroni 校正来调整多重测试。groups, Bonferroni correction was applied to adjust for multiple testing. ^ 患者层面的分析结果按患者特征分层进行分组报告。所有其他结果均使用结节水平结果报告。^ The patient-level analysis results were reported in groups stratified by patient characteristics. All other results were reported using nodule-level results. * 肺结节的诊断难度定义为低、中、高：超过三分之二、三分之一至三分之二、低于* The diagnostic difficulty of a pulmonary nodule was defined as low, intermediate, and high when more than two-thirds, between one-third and two-thirds, and less than 分别有三分之一的独立读者正确地将结节分类为良性或恶性。one-third, respectively, of unassisted readers correctly classified the nodule as benign or malignant. †临床试验中心一、二、三分别代表北京大学人民医院、武汉市第三医院、黄石市中心医院。†Clinical trial center I, II, and III represent Peking University People's Hospital, Wuhan Third Hospital, and Huangshi Central Hospital, respectively. 无辅助阅读器和 AI 辅助阅读器之间 SN 子组敏感性比较的_P_值为 0.333。a _P_value for sensitivity comparison over the SN subgroup between unassisted readers and AI-assisted readers is 0.333. b DeepFAN 和 AI 辅助阅读器之间 PSN 子组特异性比较的 _P_value 为 0.212。b _P_value for specificity comparison over the PSN subgroup between DeepFAN and AI-assisted readers is 0.212. 无辅助阅读器和 AI 辅助阅读器之间 PSN 子组特异性比较的 c _P_value 为 0.016。c _P_value for specificity comparison over the PSN subgroup between unassisted readers and AI-assisted readers is 0.016. DeepFAN 和 AI 辅助阅读器之间 GGN 子组特异性比较的 d_P_value 为 0.619。d _P_value for specificity comparison over the GGN subgroup between DeepFAN and AI-assisted readers is 0.619. DeepFAN 和 AI 辅助阅读器之间 RML 子组特异性比较的 e_P_value 为 0.023。e _P_value for specificity comparison over the RML subgroup between DeepFAN and AI-assisted readers is 0.023. 无辅助阅读器和人工智能辅助阅读器之间高诊断难度子组的敏感性比较的 f_P_值为 0.016。f _P_value for sensitivity comparison over the high diagnostic difficulty subgroup between unassisted readers and AI-assisted readers is 0.016. _缩写：_AUC = 曲线下面积，SN = 实性结节，PSN = 部分实性结节，GGN = 毛玻璃结节，RUL = 右上叶，RML = 右中叶，RLL =_Abbreviations:_AUC = area under curve, SN = solid nodule, PSN = part-solid nodule, GGN = ground-glass nodule, RUL = right upper lobe, RML = right middle lobe, RLL = 右下叶，LUL = 左上叶，LLL = 左下叶，NA = 不适用，BM = 医学学士，MM = 医学硕士，MD = 医学博士。right lower lobe, LUL = left upper lobe, LLL = left lower lobe, NA = not applicable, BM = bachelor of medicine, MM = master of medicine, MD = doctor of medicine.

当超过三分之二、三分之一至三分之二和少于三分之一的独立阅读者分别将结节正确分类为良性或恶性时，结节被定义为低、中和高。 ^ 数据被分为二元组：“不熟悉”（问卷中问题 8 的评分 ≤ 3）和“熟悉”（评分 > 3）。[+] 数据自动分为二元组：“中性”（问卷中问题 9 的评分为 3）和“大部分相信”（问卷中问题 9 的评分为 4）。[%] 与兴趣稳定性和努力持久性相关的人格特质的总毅力得分（补充表 14 和读者调查问卷）。nodule was defined as low, intermediate, and high when more than two-thirds, between one-third and two-thirds, and less than one-third, respectively, of the unassisted readers correctly classified the nodule as benign or malignant. ^ The data were categorized into binary groups: "unfamiliar" (a rating score ≤ 3 for Question 8 in the questionnaire) and "familiar" (a rating score > 3).[+] The data automatically fell into binary groups: "neutral" (a rating score of 3 for Question 9 in the questionnaire) and "mostly believe" (a rating score of 4).[%] Total grit score for personality traits related to stability of interest and persistence of effort (Supplementary Table 14 and reader questionnaire).

读者问卷调查Reader Questionnaire
在多读者多病例临床试验之前，对初级放射科医生进行了电子问卷调查。问题 1 至 13 描述了读者的个人特征Electronic questionnaires were administered to junior radiologists right before the multi-reader multi-case clinical trial. Question 1 to 13 characterize reader’s personal
特征、医学影像人工智能的经验以及对人工智能的信任态度。第 14 题和第 15 题是毅力分数的组成部分，每题由 6 个问题组成characteristics, experience with medical imaging AI, and attitude of trust toward AI. Question 14 and 15 are components of Grit score, each consisting of six questions
分别描述兴趣稳定性和努力持久性。毅力总分是通过将 12 个问题的分数相加计算得出的。问卷调查that individually describe interest stability and effort persistence. The total Grit score was calculated by summing the scores for the 12 questions. The questionnaires
最初以中文呈现。为了方便读者，我们已将中文文本翻译成英文。每个问题的数量和比例为were originally presented in Chinese. For the reader’s convenience, we have translated the Chinese text into English. The count and proportion for each question are
还提供了。also provided.
